# Current Boot Execution Carrier

Renderer full original native compilerGREEN/runtimeRED at untouched boot-axis parity law requires actual production correction. Host lifecycle/loader capabilities move into their own typed execution argument, every original caller/control/feature retained, genuine session-axis fields remain. React worker factory/service providers and WGPU wasm/worker URLs, introduction behavior, cancellation and progress are operational capabilities rather than serialized session choices. The unchanged original parity body/allowed absences remain authoritative. Locale admission uses the neutral Locale owner with an explicitly supplied lock or requested browser language tag; unsupported requested languages refuse before resource allocation. Closed language-neutral schema/fixture + independent TypeScript AST/Ajv oracle validate both capability declarations and actual boot forwarding. Complete sources before any edit:

[]) {\n      const entry = roster[domain.domain] ?? (roster[domain.domain] = { selectedByPeer: {}, hoveredByPeer: {} });\n      if (domain.selected.length > 0) entry.selectedByPeer[peer.clientId] = domain.selected;\n      if (domain.hovered.length > 0) entry.hoveredByPeer[peer.clientId] = domain.hovered;\n    }\n  }\n  return roster;\n}\n\n/** 🕹️ Peer `clientId`s with `targetId` currently selected in `domain` — empty when the roster carries no\n * peers for that domain (e.g. wave 2a's wire field not landed yet, or nobody else is online). */\nexport function peerIdsSelecting(roster: PeerInteractionRoster, domain: string, targetId: string): readonly string[] {\n  const { selectedByPeer } = roster[domain] ?? null;\n  /** 🩺️ The classified cause behind {@link PluginRuntimeState.error}, moved in lockstep with it by\n   * the one `SET_ERROR` case so a banner and its discriminator can never drift apart. */\n  readonly sessionFault: WindowFault | null;\n  /** 🩺️ A classified fault raised by a BACKGROUND command against the live instance (today the\n   * conflicts roster read), which leaves the canvas mounted — so it stamps the window bodies rather\n   * than replacing them, unlike {@link PluginRuntimeState.sessionFault}. */\n  readonly instanceFault: WindowFault | null;\n};\n\n/** 🪟️ Window UI/engagements/measures are keyed by window INSTANCE id, never by window kind — two\n * instances of the same kind (e.g. split top/perspective panes) must never share chrome or options. */\ntype WindowUiState = {\n  readonly windowUiByWindowId: Readonly<Record<string, BuiltNode>>;\n  readonly windowEngagementsByWindowId: Readonly<Record<string, WindowEngagement>>;\n  readonly windowMeasuresByWindowId: Readonly<Record<string, readonly WindowMeasure[]>>;\n  /** 🛠️ Mode-level tool measures, keyed by TOOL id (never a window id) — see `DocumentApp::tool_measures`. */\n  readonly toolMeasuresByToolId: Readonly<Record<string, readonly WindowMeasure[]>>;\n  /** 🗂️ One body store per panel key (a spawned program's under its prefixed key). The record moves only when a panel\n   * gains its store; a body that changes is loaded into that store outside render and re-renders only the mounted\n   * panel tree (ticket 26/09/23 F3: writer's Inspection body re-rendered the whole shell on every keystroke). */\n  readonly panelBodyStoreByKey: Readonly<Record<string, UiDocumentStore>>;\n  readonly appLabelsOverlay: PluginAppLabelsOverlay;\n  /** 🛍️ The active app instance's APP-STATIC operator/palette catalogue, fetched once from the reserved\n   * `framework.section.catalogue` retained surface and handed to every scene host through\n   * `AppCatalogueContext`. Never carried on a scene payload — see that context's own docstring. */\n  readonly appCatalogue: AppCatalogue;\n};\n\ntype SpawnedWindowState = {\n  /** 🪟️ Which of the focused spawned program's windows have a body, and that body's activity, keyed by the SHELL\n   * window instance id (`${spawnedId}::${windowKindId}`) — one entry per window kind the spawned app declares. The\n   * bodies themselves live only in the shell's per-window `📃️UiDocumentStore`s (`BuiltNodeStoreCacheV1`), loaded\n   * outside render, so a refresh that changes body CONTENT alone updates the mounted `UiNodeView`s and never\n   * re-renders the shell (ticket 26/09/23 F3: a puzzle3d hover re-rendered the whole shell twice, 69 ms per\n   * transition). */\n  readonly spawnedWindowActivityByWindowId: Readonly<Record<string, BuiltNode[\"activity\"]>>;\n  /** 🩺️ Why `spawnedWindowActivityByWindowId` went empty, when it went empty because the guest faulted\n   * rather than because the windows legitimately have nothing to draw — the difference an empty body\n   * cannot show. */\n  readonly spawnedWindowFault: WindowFault | null;\n  readonly spawnedWindowEngagements: Readonly<Record<string, WindowEngagement>>;\n  readonly spawnedWindowMeasures: Readonly<Record<string, readonly WindowMeasure[]>>;\n};\n\n/**\n * 🧰️ Per-window Action rail (P1–P5) state: fold/expand chrome, locally-buffered staged arg values\n * (keyed `${windowId}:${actionId}`, never dispatched until Execute), and the host-owned active utility per\n * window (never a document field, never a VCS operation). See {@link WindowActionPane}.\n */\nexport type ActionPaneState = {\n  readonly foldedByWindowId: Readonly<Record<string, boolean>>;\n  readonly expandedByWindowId: Readonly<Record<string, string | null>>;\n  readonly stagedArgsByKey: Readonly<Record<string, Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>>>;\n  readonly activeUtilityByWindowId: Readonly<Record<string, string | null>>;\n  /** 🛠️ Host-owned active tool of the active MODE (never per-window, never a document field/VCS operation) —\n   * mutually exclusive with any window's active utility. See {@link ToolRegistry}. */\n  readonly activeToolId: string | null;\n};\n\n/** 🧰️ Composite key into {@link ActionPaneState.stagedArgsByKey}. */\nexport function actionStageKey(windowId: string, actionId: string): string {\n  return `${windowId}:${actionId}`;\n}\n\nexport type ExtraWindowInstance = { readonly id: string; readonly windowKindId: string; readonly title: string };\n\n/** 🧭️ Per-anchor fold/size/active-tab-path state for one of the six {@link Panel}s. */\ntype PanelState = {\n  readonly visible: boolean;\n  readonly size: number;\n  readonly path: readonly string[];\n};\n\nfunction initialPanels(): Record<Anchor, PanelState> {\n  const panel = (): PanelState => ({ visible: false, size: DEFAULT_PANEL_WIDTH_PX, path: [] });\n  return { \"top-left\": panel(), \"top-middle\": panel(), \"top-right\": panel(), \"right-middle\": panel(), \"bottom-right\": panel(), \"bottom-middle\": panel(), \"bottom-left\": panel(), \"left-middle\": panel() };\n}\n\ntype ShellLayoutState = {\n  readonly panels: Record<Anchor, PanelState>;\n  /** 🗄️ User-rearranged dock diff against `defaultDock`, persisted via `DockLayoutStore`; `null` means \"use the computed default arrangement\". */\n  readonly dockOverride: DockSkeleton | null;\n  /** 🌱️ Per-branch drill-down memory across every anchor + mobile (see `progressPanelTabSelection`), persisted via `DockUiStateStore`. */\n  readonly panelPathMemory: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;\n  /** 🌱️ Persisted tree section/group expansion, namespaced per {@link PanelTreeUnit}, persisted via `DockUiStateStore`. */\n  readonly treeOpenStates: Readonly<Record<string, boolean>>;\n  readonly activeWindowId: string | null;\n  readonly shellLayout: WindowLayoutNode | null;\n  readonly activeExampleId: string;\n  readonly mobilePanelPath: readonly string[];\n  /** 📱️ Whether the merged mobile panel is open — dedicated state (not derived from `panels[anchor].visible`) so desktop-persisted anchor visibility never auto-opens the mobile strip on hydrate. Never persisted; mobile always boots canvas-first. */\n  readonly mobilePanelVisible: boolean;\n  readonly extraWindowInstances: readonly ExtraWindowInstance[];\n  /** 🏷️ Live window-title overrides (projection labels, etc.) keyed by window instance id — base kinds and extras. */\n  readonly windowTitlesById: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;\n  /** 🖼️ Live window-icon overrides (projection icons, etc.) keyed by window instance id — base kinds and extras. */\n  readonly windowIconsById: Readonly<Record<string, IconName>>;\n};\n\ntype OverlayState = {\n  readonly searchOpen: boolean;\n  readonly findOpen: boolean;\n  /** 🎓️ Current step of the active app's introduction walkthrough, or `null` when none is playing. */\n  readonly introductionStepIndex: number | null;\n  /** 🚦️ Introduction launch keys already auto-started during this shell lifetime — keeps a dismissed\n   * replay-on-load tour dismissed while still allowing a different app/brand introduction to launch. */\n  readonly introductionAutoStartedKeys: readonly string[];\n  /** ✅️ Indices into the active step's `interactions` that are done — reset whenever the step changes. */\n  readonly introductionCompletedInteractions: readonly number[];\n  /** 🗨️ The open declared dialog (id + `Effect`-seeded args), or `null` when none is open. */\n  readonly dialog: ShellDialogV1 | null;\n  /** 🧯️ A non-blocking, auto-dismissing notice — e.g. a `\"viewer.read-only\"` fault arriving from the\n   * host (contract freeze §2.3/§5: surfaces as a notice, never a crash). `null` when nothing to show. */\n  readonly transientNotice: TransientNotice | null;\n  /** 👁️✏️ Which role group the Document panel's \"Open with…\" section should default-expand —\n   * set by the `open-artifact-with-viewer`/`open-artifact-with-editor` palette commands (contract\n   * freeze §5) right before focusing the panel; `null` leaves both groups at their own default. */\n  readonly openWithFocusRole: AppRole | null;\n};\n\n/** 🧯️ One non-blocking shell notice — `code` carries the originating fault code (e.g.\n * `SURFACE_FAULT_CODES.ViewerReadOnly`, or `\"app.command.rejected\"` for a rejected local dispatch —\n * contract freeze `26/08/16/MUTATION-OUTCOMES-MERGE-POLICIES-AND-FIRST-CLASS-CONFLICTS` §C8/§C9)\n * when the notice was raised from a decoded {@link Fault}, `undefined` for a locally-raised notice.\n * `kind` widened from the original `\"info\" | \"error\"` to the full four-level {@link Severity}\n * vocabulary so a rejected dispatch's own worst level (`DispatchReport.worst`) can render as\n * `\"warning\"`/`\"fatal\"` too, not just collapse onto `\"error\"`. */\nexport type TransientNotice = {\n  readonly id: number;\n  readonly message: string;\n  readonly kind: Severity;\n  readonly code?: string;\n};\n\n/** 🎥️ Playback/recording state of the active `TutorialDefinition` — mutually exclusive with `overlays.introductionStepIndex` (see `SET_TUTORIAL`/`SET_INTRODUCTION_STEP` reducer cases). `playing`/`rate`/`muted`/`captionsOn`/`recording`/`deviated` are all UI-only (never persisted into the tutorial data itself). */\ntype TutorialState = {\n  readonly activeTutorialId: string | null;\n  readonly playing: boolean;\n  readonly rate: number;\n  readonly muted: boolean;\n  readonly captionsOn: boolean;\n  readonly recording: boolean;\n  /** 🧲️ True while the user has diverged from the recorded state during playback (auto-pauses); pressing play again converges the camera over `TUTORIAL_CONVERGE_MS` before resuming. */\n  readonly deviated: boolean;\n};\n\n/** 🎥️ Precomputed, shell-native restore point for `APPLY_TUTORIAL_UI_SNAPSHOT` — every cross-slice lookup (window-kind labels, layout conversion) is resolved by the caller (`applyTutorialUiSnapshotToShell`, which has `session`/`appLabelsOverlay` in scope) so every slice reducer stays pure/local. */\ntype TutorialShellUiSnapshot = {\n  readonly activeWindowId: string | null;\n  readonly shellLayout: WindowLayoutNode | null;\n  readonly extraWindowInstances: readonly ExtraWindowInstance[];\n  readonly panelPatches: Partial<Record<Anchor, { readonly visible: boolean; readonly path: readonly string[] }>>;\n  readonly treeOpenStates: Readonly<Record<string, boolean>>;\n  readonly activeUtilityByWindowId: Readonly<Record<string, string | null>>;\n  readonly activeToolId: string | null;\n  readonly dialog: ShellDialogV1 | null;\n  readonly commandPanelOpen: boolean;\n};\n\ntype UiPrefsState = {\n  readonly uiAppearance: ElementsSurfaceAppearance;\n  readonly uiLayout: UiChromeLayout;\n  readonly uiDriverId: string;\n  readonly uiCustomDrivers: Record<string, UiDriver>;\n  readonly uiDriverDraft: UiDriver | null;\n  readonly uiLocale: UiLocale;\n  readonly uiTerminology: string;\n  readonly uiThemeId: string;\n  readonly uiCustomThemes: Record<string, UiTheme>;\n  readonly uiThemeDraft: UiTheme | null;\n  readonly uiKeybindingOverrides: Record<string, string>;\n};\n\ntype SyncState = {\n  readonly syncBackboneUri: string | null;\n  readonly syncCardKind: SyncCardKind | null;\n  readonly syncDraftPath: string;\n  /** 🚦️ Per-document sync health fed by `🟦️backbone-🟦️worker.ts`'s `DocumentEvent::Status` events, keyed by `documentId`. */\n  readonly syncStatusByDocumentId: Readonly<Record<string, ArtifactSyncStatus>>;\n};\n\n/** 💡️ Host-owned ephemeral inference-port slice, keyed by the exact hub document runtime key. It\n * is fed solely by `🏪️store/👷️worker/🟦️.ts`'s\n * `inference-port-status` responses and by the shell's own operator intents; nothing in it is ever\n * persisted, and the proposal itself reaches a document only through the hub's server-stamped\n * approval command. */\ntype InferenceState = {\n  readonly portByRuntimeKey: Readonly<Record<string, InstalledServiceStatusV1>>;\n  /** 🎫️ The operation epoch that currently owns the worker-side port, or `null` when none does. */\n  readonly operationEpoch: number | null;\n  /** 🗂️ The document runtime key the live operation belongs to, or `null` when none does. */\n  readonly operationRuntimeKey: string | null;\n};\n\n/** ⚖️ Merge-outcome/first-class-conflict slice (contract freeze `26/08/16/MUTATION-OUTCOMES-MERGE-\n * POLICIES-AND-FIRST-CLASS-CONFLICTS` §C3/§C5/§C9) — `mergePolicy` mirrors the persisted\n * `os.config.merge-policy` setting (`🎚️config/🧬️schema/🧬️mutations/🛡️change-merge-policy`),\n * `conflicts` is this authority's `Conflict` roster as of the last `MergeReport`/`Conflicts` frame,\n * `selectedConflictId` drives the `📌️ChromePanels` Conflicts panel's `🔺️DiffViewHost` preview. Whether a\n * peer batch is currently quarantined is DERIVED (see {@link selectQuarantinedConflicts}), never a\n * separate stored flag — it is exactly \"any `Open` `Quarantined` conflict in this list\". */\ntype MergeState = {\n  readonly mergePolicy: MergePolicy;\n  readonly conflicts: readonly Conflict[];\n  readonly selectedConflictId: ConflictId | null;\n};\n\n/**\n * 🎛️ Command palette state not already covered by the generic per-anchor `Panel` state: the command\n * whose arg form is expanded one level above the command list (exclusive — only one at a time), and\n * locally-buffered staged arg values (never dispatched until Execute). Which category is active/folded\n * lives in `layout.panels[\"bottom-middle\"]` like any other anchor — see `buildCommandCategoryTabs`.\n */\nexport type CommandPanelState = {\n  readonly expandedCommandId: string | null;\n  readonly stagedArgsByCommandId: Readonly<Record<string, Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>>>;\n};\n\nexport type ShellState = {\n  readonly pluginRuntime: PluginRuntimeState;\n  readonly windowUi: WindowUiState;\n  readonly spawnedWindow: SpawnedWindowState;\n  readonly actionPane: ActionPaneState;\n  readonly commandPanel: CommandPanelState;\n  readonly layout: ShellLayoutState;\n  readonly overlays: OverlayState;\n  readonly tutorial: TutorialState;\n  readonly uiPrefs: UiPrefsState;\n  readonly sync: SyncState;\n  readonly inference: InferenceState;\n  readonly merge: MergeState;\n};\n//#endregion slice shapes\n\n//#region actions\n/** 🌀️ A `useState`-style value-or-updater payload, kept so every migrated `setXxx` call-site can dispatch its existing `value` or `(prev) => next` argument unchanged. */\ntype Updatable<T> = T | ((prev: T) => T);\n\nconst resolveUpdatable = <T,>(next: Updatable<T>, prev: T): T => (typeof next === \"function\" ? (next as (prev: T) => T)(prev) : next);\n\n/** 🪞️ Writes one field of a slice, keeping the slice's IDENTITY when the value did not change. Every\n * slice reducer used to spread a fresh object on every action, so `shellReducer` returned a new\n * `ShellState` for every dispatch — even the eight identity-preserving dispatches one guest refresh\n * fans out (`mergeRecordPreservingIdentity` kept the entries, the spread threw the bail-out away) —\n * and `useReducer` re-rendered the whole shell tree on every hover echo, measured at ~20 ms of chrome\n * reconcile per pointer move. A same-value write is now a no-op React bails out of. */\nfunction withField<S extends object, K extends keyof S>(state: S, key: K, value: S[K]): S {\n  return Object.is(state[key], value) ? state : { ...state, [key]: value };\n}\n\nexport type ShellAction =\n  | { readonly type: \"UPSERT_LOADED_PLUGIN\"; readonly value: LoadedProgramState }\n  | { readonly type: \"REMOVE_LOADED_PLUGIN\"; readonly pluginId: string }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_PLUGIN_STATUS\"; readonly pluginId: string; readonly value: PluginPanelStatus }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_PLUGIN_SUPERVISOR\"; readonly pluginId: string; readonly value: PluginSupervisorState }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_SESSION\"; readonly value: Updatable<ActiveSession | null> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_ERROR\"; readonly value: Updatable<string | null>; readonly fault?: WindowFault | null }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_INSTANCE_FAULT\"; readonly value: WindowFault | null }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_WINDOW_UI_BY_WINDOW_ID\"; readonly value: Updatable<Readonly<Record<string, BuiltNode>>> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_WINDOW_ENGAGEMENTS_BY_WINDOW_ID\"; readonly value: Updatable<Readonly<Record<string, WindowEngagement>>> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_WINDOW_MEASURES_BY_WINDOW_ID\"; readonly value: Updatable<Readonly<Record<string, readonly WindowMeasure[]>>> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_PANEL_BODY_STORE_BY_KEY\"; readonly value: Updatable<Readonly<Record<string, UiDocumentStore>>> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_APP_LABELS_OVERLAY\"; readonly value: Updatable<PluginAppLabelsOverlay> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_APP_CATALOGUE\"; readonly value: Updatable<AppCatalogue> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_SPAWNED_WINDOW_ACTIVITY\"; readonly value: Updatable<Readonly<Record<string, BuiltNode[\"activity\"]>>>; readonly fault?: WindowFault | null }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_SPAWNED_WINDOW_ENGAGEMENTS\"; readonly value: Updatable<Readonly<Record<string, WindowEngagement>>> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_SPAWNED_WINDOW_MEASURES\"; readonly value: Updatable<Readonly<Record<string, readonly WindowMeasure[]>>> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_ACTION_PANE_FOLDED\"; readonly windowId: string; readonly value: boolean }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_ACTION_PANE_EXPANDED\"; readonly windowId: string; readonly value: string | null }\n  | { readonly type: \"STAGE_ACTION_ARG\"; readonly windowId: string; readonly actionId: string; readonly argId: string; readonly value: unknown }\n  | { readonly type: \"RESET_ACTION_ARGS\"; readonly windowId: string; readonly actionId: string }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_ACTIVE_UTILITY\"; readonly windowId: string; readonly utilityId: string | null }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_ACTIVE_TOOL\"; readonly toolId: string | null }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_TOOL_MEASURES_BY_TOOL_ID\"; readonly value: Updatable<Readonly<Record<string, readonly WindowMeasure[]>>> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_COMMAND_EXPANDED\"; readonly value: string | null }\n  | { readonly type: \"STAGE_COMMAND_ARG\"; readonly commandId: string; readonly argId: string; readonly value: unknown }\n  | { readonly type: \"RESET_COMMAND_ARGS\"; readonly commandId: string }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_PANEL_VISIBLE\"; readonly anchor: Anchor; readonly value: Updatable<boolean> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_PANEL_SIZE\"; readonly anchor: Anchor; readonly value: Updatable<number> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_PANEL_PATH\"; readonly anchor: Anchor; readonly value: Updatable<readonly string[]> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_DOCK_OVERRIDE\"; readonly value: DockSkeleton | null }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_PANEL_PATH_MEMORY\"; readonly value: Updatable<Readonly<Record<string, string>>> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_TREE_OPEN_STATE\"; readonly id: string; readonly open: boolean }\n  | { readonly type: \"HYDRATE_DOCK_UI\"; readonly value: DockUiState | null }\n  | { readonly type: \"RESET_DOCK\" }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_ACTIVE_WINDOW_ID\"; readonly value: Updatable<string | null> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_SHELL_LAYOUT\"; readonly value: Updatable<WindowLayoutNode | null> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_ACTIVE_EXAMPLE_ID\"; readonly value: Updatable<string> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_MOBILE_PANEL_PATH\"; readonly value: Updatable<readonly string[]> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_MOBILE_PANEL_VISIBLE\"; readonly value: Updatable<boolean> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_EXTRA_WINDOW_INSTANCES\"; readonly value: Updatable<readonly ExtraWindowInstance[]> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_WINDOW_TITLE\"; readonly windowId: string; readonly title: string }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_WINDOW_ICON\"; readonly windowId: string; readonly iconId: IconName }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_SEARCH_OPEN\"; readonly value: Updatable<boolean> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_FIND_OPEN\"; readonly value: Updatable<boolean> }\n  | { readonly type: \"AUTO_START_INTRODUCTION\"; readonly key: string }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_INTRODUCTION_STEP\"; readonly value: Updatable<number | null> }\n  | { readonly type: \"COMPLETE_INTRODUCTION_INTERACTION\"; readonly index: number }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_DIALOG\"; readonly value: OverlayState[\"dialog\"] }\n  | { readonly type: \"CLOSE_DIALOG\"; readonly openingId: number }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_TRANSIENT_NOTICE\"; readonly value: TransientNotice | null }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_OPEN_WITH_FOCUS_ROLE\"; readonly value: AppRole | null }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_TUTORIAL\"; readonly value: string | null }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_TUTORIAL_PLAYING\"; readonly value: Updatable<boolean> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_TUTORIAL_RATE\"; readonly value: number }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_TUTORIAL_MUTED\"; readonly value: Updatable<boolean> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_TUTORIAL_CAPTIONS\"; readonly value: Updatable<boolean> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_TUTORIAL_RECORDING\"; readonly value: boolean }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_TUTORIAL_DEVIATED\"; readonly value: boolean }\n  | { readonly type: \"APPLY_TUTORIAL_UI_SNAPSHOT\"; readonly snapshot: TutorialShellUiSnapshot }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_UI_APPEARANCE\"; readonly value: Updatable<ElementsSurfaceAppearance> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_UI_LAYOUT\"; readonly value: Updatable<UiChromeLayout> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_UI_DRIVER_ID\"; readonly value: Updatable<string> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_UI_CUSTOM_DRIVERS\"; readonly value: Updatable<Record<string, UiDriver>> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_UI_DRIVER_DRAFT\"; readonly value: Updatable<UiDriver | null> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_UI_LOCALE\"; readonly value: Updatable<UiLocale> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_UI_TERMINOLOGY\"; readonly value: Updatable<string> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_UI_THEME_ID\"; readonly value: Updatable<string> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_UI_CUSTOM_THEMES\"; readonly value: Updatable<Record<string, UiTheme>> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_UI_THEME_DRAFT\"; readonly value: Updatable<UiTheme | null> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_UI_KEYBINDING_OVERRIDES\"; readonly value: Updatable<Record<string, string>> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_SYNC_BACKBONE_URI\"; readonly value: Updatable<string | null> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_SYNC_CARD_KIND\"; readonly value: Updatable<SyncCardKind | null> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_SYNC_DRAFT_PATH\"; readonly value: Updatable<string> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_SYNC_STATUS_FOR_DOCUMENT\"; readonly documentId: string; readonly status: ArtifactSyncStatus }\n  | { readonly type: \"OPEN_INFERENCE_PORT\"; readonly runtimeKey: string; readonly operationEpoch: number }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_INFERENCE_PORT_FOR_DOCUMENT\"; readonly runtimeKey: string; readonly status: InstalledServiceStatusV1 }\n  | { readonly type: \"CLEAR_INFERENCE_PORT_FOR_DOCUMENT\"; readonly runtimeKey: string }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_MERGE_POLICY\"; readonly value: MergePolicy }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_CONFLICTS\"; readonly value: Updatable<readonly Conflict[]> }\n  | { readonly type: \"SET_SELECTED_CONFLICT_ID\"; readonly value: ConflictId | null };\n//#endregion actions\n\n//#region slice reducers\n/** 🔌️ The loaded set with `entry` in its plugin's place (appended when new) — the one upsert the reducer applies,\n * shared with callers that must read the set before the next render commits it. */\nexport function upsertLoadedProgramV1(loaded: readonly LoadedProgramState[], entry: LoadedProgramState): readonly LoadedProgramState[] {\n  const index = loaded.findIndex((candidate) => candidate.handle.pluginId === entry.handle.pluginId);\n  return index === -1 ? [...loaded, entry] : loaded.map((candidate, i) => (i === index ? entry : candidate));\n}\n\nfunction pluginRuntimeReducer(state: PluginRuntimeState, action: ShellAction): PluginRuntimeState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"UPSERT_LOADED_PLUGIN\":\n      return { ...state, loadedPlugins: upsertLoadedProgramV1(state.loadedPlugins, action.value) };\n    case \"REMOVE_LOADED_PLUGIN\":\n      return { ...state, loadedPlugins: state.loadedPlugins.filter((entry) => entry.handle.pluginId !== action.pluginId) };\n    case \"SET_PLUGIN_STATUS\":\n      return { ...state, pluginStatusById: { ...state.pluginStatusById, [action.pluginId]: action.value } };\n    case \"SET_PLUGIN_SUPERVISOR\":\n      return { ...state, pluginSupervisorById: { ...state.pluginSupervisorById, [action.pluginId]: action.value } };\n    case \"SET_SESSION\":\n      return withField(state, \"session\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.session));\n    case \"SET_ERROR\":\n      return { ...state, error: resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.error), sessionFault: action.fault ?? null };\n    case \"SET_INSTANCE_FAULT\":\n      return state.instanceFault === action.value ? state : { ...state, instanceFault: action.value };\n    default:\n      return state;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction windowUiReducer(state: WindowUiState, action: ShellAction): WindowUiState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"SET_WINDOW_UI_BY_WINDOW_ID\":\n      return withField(state, \"windowUiByWindowId\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.windowUiByWindowId));\n    case \"SET_WINDOW_ENGAGEMENTS_BY_WINDOW_ID\":\n      return withField(state, \"windowEngagementsByWindowId\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.windowEngagementsByWindowId));\n    case \"SET_WINDOW_MEASURES_BY_WINDOW_ID\":\n      return withField(state, \"windowMeasuresByWindowId\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.windowMeasuresByWindowId));\n    case \"SET_TOOL_MEASURES_BY_TOOL_ID\":\n      return withField(state, \"toolMeasuresByToolId\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.toolMeasuresByToolId));\n    case \"SET_PANEL_BODY_STORE_BY_KEY\":\n      return withField(state, \"panelBodyStoreByKey\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.panelBodyStoreByKey));\n    case \"SET_APP_LABELS_OVERLAY\":\n      return withField(state, \"appLabelsOverlay\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.appLabelsOverlay));\n    case \"SET_APP_CATALOGUE\":\n      return withField(state, \"appCatalogue\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.appCatalogue));\n    default:\n      return state;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction spawnedWindowReducer(state: SpawnedWindowState, action: ShellAction): SpawnedWindowState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"SET_SPAWNED_WINDOW_ACTIVITY\": {\n      const spawnedWindowActivityByWindowId = resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.spawnedWindowActivityByWindowId);\n      const spawnedWindowFault = action.fault ?? null;\n      return Object.is(spawnedWindowActivityByWindowId, state.spawnedWindowActivityByWindowId) && Object.is(spawnedWindowFault, state.spawnedWindowFault) ? state : { ...state, spawnedWindowActivityByWindowId, spawnedWindowFault };\n    }\n    case \"SET_SPAWNED_WINDOW_ENGAGEMENTS\":\n      return withField(state, \"spawnedWindowEngagements\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.spawnedWindowEngagements));\n    case \"SET_SPAWNED_WINDOW_MEASURES\":\n      return withField(state, \"spawnedWindowMeasures\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.spawnedWindowMeasures));\n    default:\n      return state;\n  }\n}\n\n/** 🧰️ Reducer for the per-window Action rail slice (P1–P5). Every case preserves referential identity when nothing actually changes so downstream memos can bail. */\nfunction actionPaneReducer(state: ActionPaneState, action: ShellAction): ActionPaneState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"SET_ACTION_PANE_FOLDED\": {\n      if (state.foldedByWindowId[action.windowId] === action.value) return state;\n      return { ...state, foldedByWindowId: { ...state.foldedByWindowId, [action.windowId]: action.value } };\n    }\n    case \"SET_ACTION_PANE_EXPANDED\": {\n      if ((state.expandedByWindowId[action.windowId] ?? null) === action.value) return state;\n      return { ...state, expandedByWindowId: { ...state.expandedByWindowId, [action.windowId]: action.value } };\n    }\n    case \"STAGE_ACTION_ARG\": {\n      const key = actionStageKey(action.windowId, action.actionId);\n      const current = state.stagedArgsByKey[key] ?? {};\n      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(current, action.argId) && current[action.argId] === action.value) return state;\n      return { ...state, stagedArgsByKey: { ...state.stagedArgsByKey, [key]: { ...current, [action.argId]: action.value } } };\n    }\n    case \"RESET_ACTION_ARGS\": {\n      const key = actionStageKey(action.windowId, action.actionId);\n      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.stagedArgsByKey, key)) return state;\n      const next = { ...state.stagedArgsByKey };\n      delete next[key];\n      return { ...state, stagedArgsByKey: next };\n    }\n    case \"SET_ACTIVE_UTILITY\": {\n      if ((state.activeUtilityByWindowId[action.windowId] ?? null) === action.utilityId) return state;\n      return { ...state, activeUtilityByWindowId: { ...state.activeUtilityByWindowId, [action.windowId]: action.utilityId } };\n    }\n    case \"SET_ACTIVE_TOOL\":\n      if (state.activeToolId === action.toolId) return state;\n      return { ...state, activeToolId: action.toolId };\n    case \"APPLY_TUTORIAL_UI_SNAPSHOT\":\n      return { ...state, activeUtilityByWindowId: action.snapshot.activeUtilityByWindowId, activeToolId: action.snapshot.activeToolId };\n    default:\n      return state;\n  }\n}\n\n/** 🎛️ Reducer for the command palette's arg-expansion/staging slice — category active/fold state is the `bottom-middle` anchor's own generic `SET_PANEL_VISIBLE`/`SET_PANEL_PATH` (see `shellLayoutReducer`), not handled here. */\nfunction commandPanelReducer(state: CommandPanelState, action: ShellAction): CommandPanelState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"SET_COMMAND_EXPANDED\": {\n      if (state.expandedCommandId === action.value) return state;\n      return withField(state, \"expandedCommandId\", action.value);\n    }\n    case \"STAGE_COMMAND_ARG\": {\n      const current = state.stagedArgsByCommandId[action.commandId] ?? {};\n      if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(current, action.argId) && current[action.argId] === action.value) return state;\n      return { ...state, stagedArgsByCommandId: { ...state.stagedArgsByCommandId, [action.commandId]: { ...current, [action.argId]: action.value } } };\n    }\n    case \"RESET_COMMAND_ARGS\": {\n      if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.stagedArgsByCommandId, action.commandId)) return state;\n      const next = { ...state.stagedArgsByCommandId };\n      delete next[action.commandId];\n      return { ...state, stagedArgsByCommandId: next };\n    }\n    default:\n      return state;\n  }\n}\n\n/** 🏷️ Updates the named leaf while retaining every unaffected branch and its layout geometry. */\nfunction withWindowLayoutTitle(node: WindowLayoutNode, windowId: string, title: string): WindowLayoutNode {\n  if (node.kind === \"window\") return node.id === windowId && node.title !== title ? { ...node, title: uiDataLabel(title) } : node;\n  const children = node.children.map(child => withWindowLayoutTitle(child, windowId, title));\n  return children.some((child, index) => child !== node.children[index]) ? { ...node, children } as WindowLayoutNode : node;\n}\n\nfunction shellLayoutReducer(state: ShellLayoutState, action: ShellAction): ShellLayoutState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"SET_PANEL_VISIBLE\":\n      return { ...state, panels: { ...state.panels, [action.anchor]: { ...state.panels[action.anchor], visible: resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.panels[action.anchor].visible) } } };\n    case \"SET_PANEL_SIZE\":\n      return { ...state, panels: { ...state.panels, [action.anchor]: { ...state.panels[action.anchor], size: resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.panels[action.anchor].size) } } };\n    case \"SET_PANEL_PATH\":\n      return { ...state, panels: { ...state.panels, [action.anchor]: { ...state.panels[action.anchor], path: resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.panels[action.anchor].path) } } };\n    case \"SET_DOCK_OVERRIDE\":\n      return withField(state, \"dockOverride\", action.value);\n    case \"SET_PANEL_PATH_MEMORY\":\n      return withField(state, \"panelPathMemory\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.panelPathMemory));\n    case \"SET_TREE_OPEN_STATE\":\n      return { ...state, treeOpenStates: { ...state.treeOpenStates, [action.id]: action.open } };\n    case \"HYDRATE_DOCK_UI\": {\n      if (!action.value) return state;\n      const panels = { ...state.panels };\n      for (const anchor of ANCHORS) {\n        const saved = action.value.anchors[anchor];\n        if (!saved) continue;\n        panels[anchor] = {\n          visible: saved.visible ?? panels[anchor].visible,\n          size: saved.size ?? panels[anchor].size,\n          path: saved.path ?? panels[anchor].path,\n        };\n      }\n      return { ...state, panels, panelPathMemory: action.value.pathMemory ?? state.panelPathMemory, treeOpenStates: action.value.treeOpen ?? state.treeOpenStates };\n    }\n    case \"RESET_DOCK\": {\n      const panels = {} as Record<Anchor, PanelState>;\n      for (const anchor of ANCHORS) panels[anchor] = { visible: false, size: DEFAULT_PANEL_WIDTH_PX, path: [] };\n      return { ...state, dockOverride: null, panels, panelPathMemory: {}, treeOpenStates: {} };\n    }\n    case \"SET_ACTIVE_WINDOW_ID\":\n      return withField(state, \"activeWindowId\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.activeWindowId));\n    case \"SET_SHELL_LAYOUT\":\n      return withField(state, \"shellLayout\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.shellLayout));\n    case \"SET_ACTIVE_EXAMPLE_ID\":\n      return withField(state, \"activeExampleId\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.activeExampleId));\n    case \"SET_MOBILE_PANEL_PATH\":\n      return withField(state, \"mobilePanelPath\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.mobilePanelPath));\n    case \"SET_MOBILE_PANEL_VISIBLE\":\n      return withField(state, \"mobilePanelVisible\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.mobilePanelVisible));\n    case \"SET_EXTRA_WINDOW_INSTANCES\":\n      return withField(state, \"extraWindowInstances\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.extraWindowInstances));\n    case \"SET_WINDOW_TITLE\": {\n      const windowTitlesById = { ...state.windowTitlesById, [action.windowId]: action.title };\n      const extraWindowInstances = state.extraWindowInstances.map((entry) => (entry.id === action.windowId ? { ...entry, title: action.title } : entry));\n      const shellLayout = state.shellLayout ? withWindowLayoutTitle(state.shellLayout, action.windowId, action.title) : null;\n      return { ...state, windowTitlesById, extraWindowInstances, shellLayout };\n    }\n    case \"SET_WINDOW_ICON\": {\n      const windowIconsById = { ...state.windowIconsById, [action.windowId]: action.iconId };\n      return { ...state, windowIconsById };\n    }\n    case \"APPLY_TUTORIAL_UI_SNAPSHOT\": {\n      const { snapshot } = action;\n      const panels = { ...state.panels };\n      for (const anchor of ANCHORS) {\n        const patch = snapshot.panelPatches[anchor];\n        if (!patch) continue;\n        panels[anchor] = { ...panels[anchor], visible: patch.visible, path: patch.path };\n      }\n      return { ...state, activeWindowId: snapshot.activeWindowId, shellLayout: snapshot.shellLayout, extraWindowInstances: snapshot.extraWindowInstances, treeOpenStates: snapshot.treeOpenStates, panels };\n    }\n    default:\n      return state;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction overlayReducer(state: OverlayState, action: ShellAction): OverlayState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"SET_SEARCH_OPEN\":\n      return withField(state, \"searchOpen\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.searchOpen));\n    case \"SET_FIND_OPEN\":\n      return withField(state, \"findOpen\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.findOpen));\n    case \"AUTO_START_INTRODUCTION\":\n      if (state.introductionAutoStartedKeys.includes(action.key)) return state;\n      return {\n        ...state,\n        introductionStepIndex: 0,\n        introductionAutoStartedKeys: [...state.introductionAutoStartedKeys, action.key],\n        introductionCompletedInteractions: [],\n      };\n    case \"SET_INTRODUCTION_STEP\": {\n      const nextIndex = resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.introductionStepIndex);\n      return { ...state, introductionStepIndex: nextIndex, introductionCompletedInteractions: [] };\n    }\n    case \"COMPLETE_INTRODUCTION_INTERACTION\":\n      return state.introductionCompletedInteractions.includes(action.index)\n        ? state\n        : { ...state, introductionCompletedInteractions: [...state.introductionCompletedInteractions, action.index] };\n    // 🎥️ Starting a tutorial (a non-null id) is mutually exclusive with an active introduction — mirrors\n    // the `SET_INTRODUCTION_STEP` case below clearing the tutorial slice.\n    case \"SET_TUTORIAL\":\n      return action.value != null && state.introductionStepIndex != null ? { ...state, introductionStepIndex: null, introductionCompletedInteractions: [] } : state;\n    case \"SET_DIALOG\":\n      return withField(state, \"dialog\", action.value);\n    case \"CLOSE_DIALOG\":\n      return state.dialog?.openingId === action.openingId ? { ...state, dialog: null } : state;\n    case \"SET_TRANSIENT_NOTICE\":\n      return withField(state, \"transientNotice\", action.value);\n    case \"SET_OPEN_WITH_FOCUS_ROLE\":\n      return withField(state, \"openWithFocusRole\", action.value);\n    case \"APPLY_TUTORIAL_UI_SNAPSHOT\":\n      return { ...state, dialog: action.snapshot.dialog, searchOpen: action.snapshot.commandPanelOpen };\n    default:\n      return state;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction uiPrefsReducer(state: UiPrefsState, action: ShellAction): UiPrefsState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"SET_UI_APPEARANCE\":\n      return withField(state, \"uiAppearance\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.uiAppearance));\n    case \"SET_UI_LAYOUT\":\n      return withField(state, \"uiLayout\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.uiLayout));\n    case \"SET_UI_DRIVER_ID\":\n      return withField(state, \"uiDriverId\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.uiDriverId));\n    case \"SET_UI_CUSTOM_DRIVERS\":\n      return withField(state, \"uiCustomDrivers\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.uiCustomDrivers));\n    case \"SET_UI_DRIVER_DRAFT\":\n      return withField(state, \"uiDriverDraft\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.uiDriverDraft));\n    case \"SET_UI_LOCALE\":\n      return withField(state, \"uiLocale\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.uiLocale));\n    case \"SET_UI_TERMINOLOGY\":\n      return withField(state, \"uiTerminology\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.uiTerminology));\n    case \"SET_UI_THEME_ID\":\n      return withField(state, \"uiThemeId\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.uiThemeId));\n    case \"SET_UI_CUSTOM_THEMES\":\n      return withField(state, \"uiCustomThemes\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.uiCustomThemes));\n    case \"SET_UI_THEME_DRAFT\":\n      return withField(state, \"uiThemeDraft\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.uiThemeDraft));\n    case \"SET_UI_KEYBINDING_OVERRIDES\":\n      return withField(state, \"uiKeybindingOverrides\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.uiKeybindingOverrides));\n    default:\n      return state;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction syncReducer(state: SyncState, action: ShellAction): SyncState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"SET_SYNC_BACKBONE_URI\":\n      return withField(state, \"syncBackboneUri\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.syncBackboneUri));\n    case \"SET_SYNC_CARD_KIND\":\n      return withField(state, \"syncCardKind\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.syncCardKind));\n    case \"SET_SYNC_DRAFT_PATH\":\n      return withField(state, \"syncDraftPath\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.syncDraftPath));\n    case \"SET_SYNC_STATUS_FOR_DOCUMENT\":\n      return { ...state, syncStatusByDocumentId: { ...state.syncStatusByDocumentId, [action.documentId]: action.status } };\n    default:\n      return state;\n  }\n}\n\n/** 💡️ Reducer for the host-owned ephemeral inference-port slice — see {@link InferenceState}. It\n * never invents a phase: `OPEN_INFERENCE_PORT` seeds exactly `idle`, every later phase arrives as an\n * exact worker answer, and a cleared document leaves no residue. */\nfunction inferenceReducer(state: InferenceState, action: ShellAction): InferenceState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"OPEN_INFERENCE_PORT\":\n      return { portByRuntimeKey: { [action.runtimeKey]: idleInstalledServiceStatusV1() }, operationEpoch: action.operationEpoch, operationRuntimeKey: action.runtimeKey };\n    case \"SET_INFERENCE_PORT_FOR_DOCUMENT\":\n      return state.operationRuntimeKey === action.runtimeKey ? { ...state, portByRuntimeKey: { [action.runtimeKey]: action.status } } : state;\n    case \"CLEAR_INFERENCE_PORT_FOR_DOCUMENT\": {\n      if (!(action.runtimeKey in state.portByRuntimeKey)) return state;\n      const portByRuntimeKey = { ...state.portByRuntimeKey };\n      delete portByRuntimeKey[action.runtimeKey];\n      const owned = state.operationRuntimeKey === action.runtimeKey;\n      return { portByRuntimeKey, operationEpoch: owned ? null : state.operationEpoch, operationRuntimeKey: owned ? null : state.operationRuntimeKey };\n    }\n    default:\n      return state;\n  }\n}\n\n/** ⚖️ Reducer for the merge-outcome/first-class-conflict slice — see {@link MergeState}. */\nfunction mergeReducer(state: MergeState, action: ShellAction): MergeState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"SET_MERGE_POLICY\":\n      return state.mergePolicy === action.value ? state : { ...state, mergePolicy: action.value };\n    case \"SET_CONFLICTS\": {\n      const conflicts = resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.conflicts);\n      const selectedConflictId = state.selectedConflictId && conflicts.some((entry) => entry.id === state.selectedConflictId) ? state.selectedConflictId : null;\n      return { ...state, conflicts, selectedConflictId };\n    }\n    case \"SET_SELECTED_CONFLICT_ID\":\n      return state.selectedConflictId === action.value ? state : { ...state, selectedConflictId: action.value };\n    default:\n      return state;\n  }\n}\n\n/** 🎥️ Reducer for the tutorial playback/recording slice — see `TutorialState`. `SET_TUTORIAL` resets every playback-rate/deviation flag (a fresh tutorial never inherits the previous one's rate/deviation); starting an introduction (`SET_INTRODUCTION_STEP` with a non-null value) clears the active tutorial, mirroring `overlayReducer`'s reverse case. */\nfunction tutorialReducer(state: TutorialState, action: ShellAction): TutorialState {\n  switch (action.type) {\n    case \"SET_TUTORIAL\":\n      return { activeTutorialId: action.value, playing: false, rate: 1, muted: state.muted, captionsOn: state.captionsOn, recording: false, deviated: false };\n    case \"SET_TUTORIAL_PLAYING\": {\n      const nextPlaying = resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.playing);\n      return { ...state, playing: nextPlaying, deviated: nextPlaying ? false : state.deviated };\n    }\n    case \"SET_TUTORIAL_RATE\":\n      return withField(state, \"rate\", action.value);\n    case \"SET_TUTORIAL_MUTED\":\n      return withField(state, \"muted\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.muted));\n    case \"SET_TUTORIAL_CAPTIONS\":\n      return withField(state, \"captionsOn\", resolveUpdatable(action.value, state.captionsOn));\n    case \"SET_TUTORIAL_RECORDING\":\n      return withField(state, \"recording\", action.value);\n    case \"SET_TUTORIAL_DEVIATED\":\n      return withField(state, \"deviated\", action.value);\n    // 🎓️ Only literal (non-updater) values can be checked here without this slice's own prior value —\n    // every real call site dispatches a literal step index or `null` (never a functional updater), so\n    // this conservatively no-ops on the (currently unused) updater form rather than guessing.\n    case \"SET_INTRODUCTION_STEP\":\n      return typeof action.value !== \"function\" && action.value != null && state.activeTutorialId != null ? { ...state, activeTutorialId: null, playing: false, deviated: false } : state;\n    default:\n      return state;\n  }\n}\n\n//#endregion slice reducers\n\n//#region 🕹️LocalInteraction\n/** 🕹️ The shell's own local interaction (selection, hover, mode and granularity per domain) — EPHEMERAL LOCAL state that\n * nothing the shell renders reads: the leftover inspection and the tutorial capture/replay read it through `get`, the\n * tutorial recorder through `subscribe`. It is no `ShellState` slice because a slice change re-renders the whole shell,\n * and every world hover publishes one (ticket 26/09/23 F3: the last whole-shell render per puzzle3d hover transition). */\nexport type LocalInteractionStoreV1 = {\n  readonly get: () => InteractionState;\n  readonly observe: (state: InteractionState) => void;\n  readonly subscribe: (listener: () => void) => () => void;\n};\n\n/** 🧲️ An observation is a freshly decoded guest read, so it is structurally shared with the current state: an unchanged\n * one keeps the identity and notifies nobody, a hover-only one keeps every other field's identity. */\nexport function createLocalInteractionStoreV1(initial: InteractionState = EMPTY_INTERACTION_STATE): LocalInteractionStoreV1 {\n  let current = initial;\n  const listeners = new Set<() => void>();\n  return {\n    get: () => current,\n    observe: (state) => {\n      const next = mergeRecordPreservingIdentity(current, Object.entries(state)) as InteractionState;\n      if (next === current) return;\n      current = next;\n      for (const listener of [...listeners]) listener();\n    },\n    subscribe: (listener) => {\n      listeners.add(listener);\n      return () => {\n        listeners.delete(listener);\n      };\n    },\n  };\n}\n//#endregion 🕹️LocalInteraction\n\n/** 🧵️ Root reducer for `FrameworkOsShell` — fans every action out to its owning slice reducer; slices that ignore an action's type return their input unchanged, so unrelated slices keep referential identity. */\nexport function shellReducer(state: ShellState, action: ShellAction): ShellState {\n  const pluginRuntime = pluginRuntimeReducer(state.pluginRuntime, action);\n  const previousSession = state.pluginRuntime.session;\n  const nextSession = pluginRuntime.session;\n  const ownerChanged = previousSession?.pluginId !== nextSession?.pluginId || previousSession?.instanceId !== nextSession?.instanceId || previousSession?.app.id !== nextSession?.app.id;\n  const layout = shellLayoutReducer(state.layout, action);\n  const next: ShellState = {\n    pluginRuntime,\n    windowUi: windowUiReducer(state.windowUi, action),\n    spawnedWindow: spawnedWindowReducer(state.spawnedWindow, action),\n    actionPane: actionPaneReducer(state.actionPane, action),\n    commandPanel: commandPanelReducer(state.commandPanel, action),\n    layout: ownerChanged ? { ...layout, windowTitlesById: {}, windowIconsById: {} } : layout,\n    overlays: overlayReducer(state.overlays, action),\n    tutorial: tutorialReducer(state.tutorial, action),\n    uiPrefs: uiPrefsReducer(state.uiPrefs, action),\n    sync: syncReducer(state.sync, action),\n    inference: inferenceReducer(state.inference, action),\n    merge: mergeReducer(state.merge, action),\n  };\n  // 🪞️ An action no slice answered with a new value is a no-op: return the SAME state so\n  // `useReducer` bails out instead of reconciling the whole shell (see `withField`).\n  return shellStateUnchanged(state, next) ? state : next;\n}\n\n/** 🪞️ True when every slice kept its identity. */\nexport function shellStateUnchanged(previous: ShellState, next: ShellState): boolean {\n  return (Object.keys(next) as (keyof ShellState)[]).every((key) => Object.is(previous[key], next[key]));\n}\n\n//#region selectors\n/**\n * 📱️ The device this shell paints for: the MEASURED viewport device wins whenever it is narrower than a\n * desktop, and only a desktop-width viewport falls through to the user's stored `uiLayout` preference.\n *\n * `measured` comes from `useUiDevice()` — `🖱️ui/📱️device/🟦️.ts`'s three-way policy, the same thresholds\n * the wgpu dock compares its `screen_w` against. It used to be a bare `mobile: boolean`, so the 768-1023\n * band painted the DESKTOP layout and `\"tablet\"` was a value only a user who found the settings row ever\n * saw, contradicting AGENTS.md's explicit desktop → mobile → tablet priority. Pinning `\"tablet\"` in\n * settings still works, and still only affects a desktop-width viewport (pinning a wider layout on a\n * phone is exactly the case that has no room for it).\n */\nexport const selectUiDevice = (state: ShellState, measured: ElementsSurfaceDevice): ElementsSurfaceDevice => (measured === \"desktop\" ? state.uiPrefs.uiLayout : measured);\n\n/** ⚖️ Open `Quarantined` conflicts — \"a peer batch is being held\" (contract freeze §C6/§C9's\n * `ShellSync` quarantine indicator) is exactly \"this list is non-empty\", never a separately stored\n * flag (see {@link MergeState}'s doc comment). */\nexport const selectQuarantinedConflicts = (state: ShellState): readonly Conflict[] => state.merge.conflicts.filter((conflict) => conflict.kind.kind === \"quarantined\" && conflict.status === \"open\");\n\n/** ⚖️ Open conflicts of either kind, for the `📌️ChromePanels` Conflicts panel. */\nexport const selectOpenConflicts = (state: ShellState): readonly Conflict[] => state.merge.conflicts.filter((conflict) => conflict.status === \"open\");\n//#endregion selectors\n\n/** 🌱️ Builds the starting `ShellState` for `FrameworkOsShell`, mirroring exactly what each migrated `useState` used to initialize to (including reads from the shell's own storage for UI prefs). */\nexport function initialShellState(_props: {\n  readonly pluginFilter?: string;\n  readonly plugins: readonly { readonly pluginId: string; readonly moduleUrl: string }[];\n  readonly locks?: ResolvedShellLocks;\n  readonly defaults?: FrameworkOsDefaults;\n  readonly storage: StoragePort;\n}): ShellState {\n  const locks = _props.locks ?? {};\n  const defaults = _props.defaults ?? {};\n  const storage = _props.storage;\n  const preferences = resolveUiPreferences(readUiPreferences(storage), {\n    appearance: \"system\",\n    layout: \"desktop\",\n    driverId: \"default\",\n    locale: uiI18n.resolvedLanguage?.toLowerCase().startsWith(\"de\") ?     "before": "import { admitWgpuPluginModules } from \"../🧩️plugin-modules/🛂️admission/🟦️.ts\";\n// #region 🧲️Header\n/** 🧊️ `@semio-tech/framework-renderer-wgpu` — raw wgpu WASM renderer boot for declarative Rust program UI trees. */\n// #endregion 🧲️Header\n\nimport { resolveWgpuBootDescriptor, type WgpuBootDefaults, type WgpuBootHub, type WgpuBootLocks } from \"../🧭️boot-descriptor/🟦️.ts\";\nimport { mountWgpuBrowserHost, type WgpuBrowserMount, type WgpuBootProgress } from \"../🌐️browser-host/🟦️.ts\";\n\n/** 🧊️ The embeddable wgpu boot door. Field-for-field React's `FrameworkOsBootOptions`\n * (`🧱️elements/🐚️Shell/🟦️.tsx`), so one call site can swap renderers without dropping options: it\n * used to carry `rootId`/`plugin`/`plugins`/`rendererModuleUrl` alone, which silently discarded\n * `appId`/`appRole`/`locks`/`defaults`/`brand` on this target. [], window.location.href);\n  const descriptor = resolveWgpuBootDescriptor({\n    hash: window.location.hash, defaultVariant: plugins[0]?.pluginId ?? DEFAULT_EMBEDDED_VARIANT,\n    overrides: { plugin: options.plugin, appId: options.appId, appRole: options.appRole, appMode: options.appMode, appExample: options.appExample, brandId: options.brand, locks: options.locks, defaults: options.defaults, hub: options.hub },\n  });\n  const moduleUrl = new URL(options.rendererModuleUrl ?? DEFAULT_RENDERER_MODULE_URL, window.location.href).href;\n  const wasmUrl = options.rendererWasmUrl ?? moduleUrl.replace(/\\.js(?=[?#]|$)/u, \"_bg.wasm\");\n  const locale = descriptor.locks.locale === \"de\" ? \"de\" : descriptor.locks.locale === \"en\" ? \"en\" : navigator.language.toLowerCase().startsWith(\"de\") ? \"de\" : \"en\";\n  return mountWgpuBrowserHost(root, { descriptor, locale, mountId: rootId, rendererModuleUrl: moduleUrl, rendererWasmUrl: wasmUrl, plugins, pluginRegistrySelection: options.plugin === undefined ? \"all\" : \"variant\", frameWorkerUrl: options.frameWorkerUrl, suppressAutoIntroduction: options.suppressAutoIntroduction, signal: options.signal, onProgress: options.onProgress });\n}\n\nexport type { WgpuBootProgress } from \"../🌐️browser-host/🟦️.ts\";\n"

    "path": "🧰️framework/🛍️products/💻️os/🔨️modules/📺️renderer/🧑‍🎨engine/🎯️targets/🧊️wgpu/🚀️browser-boot/🟦️.ts",
    "before": "import { DEFAULT_PLAYGROUND_VARIANT } from \"../../../../../🔌️plugin/📇️registry/🤖️generated/🎮️playgrounds/🟦️.ts\";\nimport { documentBootMetaReader, resolveWgpuBootDescriptor, stripBootBrokerProof, wgpuReadinessBeacon, WGPU_READINESS_BEACON_UNKNOWN_PLUGIN } from \"../🧭️boot-descriptor/🟦️.ts\";\nimport { mountWgpuBrowserHost } from \"../🌐️browser-host/🟦️.ts\";\n\nexport { WGPU_CANVAS_ID, WGPU_INTROSPECTION_GLOBAL, WGPU_HUB_PROJECTION_GLOBAL, WGPU_ACCESSIBILITY_MIRROR_ID } from \"../🌐️browser-host/🟦️.ts\";\n\nawait new Promise<void>(resolve => {\n  if (document.readyState === \"loading\") document.addEventListener(\"DOMContentLoaded\", () => resolve(), { once: true });\n  else resolve();\n});\nconst root = document.getElementById(\"root\");\nif (!root) throw new Error(\"missing-root: #root is unavailable\");\ntry {\n  const descriptor = resolveWgpuBootDescriptor({ search: window.location.search, hash: window.location.hash, meta: documentBootMetaReader(document), defaultVariant: DEFAULT_PLAYGROUND_VARIANT });\n  stripBootBrokerProof(window.location, window.history);\n  const locale = descriptor.locks.locale === \"de\" ? \"de\" : descriptor.locks.locale === \"en\" ? \"en\" : navigator.language.toLowerCase().startsWith(\"de\") ? \"de\" : \"en\";\n  await mountWgpuBrowserHost(root, { descriptor, locale, mountId: \"root\", pageBindings: true });\n} catch (error) {\n  const detail = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n  wgpuReadinessBeacon(document.documentElement, WGPU_READINESS_BEACON_UNKNOWN_PLUGIN).error();\n  if (!root.querySelector('[role=\"alert\"]')) {\n    const banner = document.createElement(\"div\");\n    banner.setAttribute(\"role\", \"alert\");\n    banner.textContent = detail;\n    root.append(banner);\n    console.error(\"wgpu renderer fault: worker-boot-failed: \" + detail);\n  }\n  throw error;\n}\n"

    "path": "🧰️framework/🛍️products/💻️os/🔨️modules/📺️renderer/🧑‍🎨engine/🎯️targets/🧊️wgpu/🧪️tests/🪆️embedded-browser/🟦️.ts",
    "before": "import { strict as assert } from \"node:assert\";\nimport { mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from \"node:fs\";\nimport { resolve } from \"node:path\";\nimport fixture from \"../../../../🧫️fixtures/🪆️embedded-browser/🔣️.json\";\n\ntype Configuration = { renderer: \"react\" | \"wgpu\"; serve: string; libraryModuleUrl: string; styleModuleUrl?: string; sessionFactoryModules?: { moduleUrl: string; exportName: string }[]; rendererModuleUrl?: string; rendererWasmUrl?: string; frameWorkerUrl?: string; roots: { rootId: string; plugins: { pluginId: string; moduleUrl: string }[] }[] };\ntype State = { id: string; locale: string; ready: boolean; error: string | null; intro: boolean; palette: boolean; controls: { id: string; rect: number[] }[]; surfaces: string[]; text: string; canvases: number; layout: number[] };\ntype Receipt = { phase: string; before: State[]; after: State[]; workers: number; input: unknown[]; evidence?: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> };\n\n/** 🪆️ Requires explicit per-root plugin authority and real public browser module URLs. */\nexport function parseEmbeddedBrowserConfiguration(value: unknown): Configuration {\n  assert.ok(value && typeof value === \"object\" && !Array.isArray(value));\n  const c = value as Configuration;\n  assert.ok(c.renderer === \"react\" || c.renderer === \"wgpu\");\n  const allowed = [\"renderer\", \"serve\", \"libraryModuleUrl\", \"styleModuleUrl\", \"sessionFactoryModules\", \"rendererModuleUrl\", \"rendererWasmUrl\", \"frameWorkerUrl\", \"roots\"];\n  assert.ok(Object.keys(c).every(k => allowed.includes(k)));\n  for (const key of [\"serve\", \"libraryModuleUrl\", ...(c.renderer === \"wgpu\" ? [\"rendererModuleUrl\", \"rendererWasmUrl\", \"frameWorkerUrl\"] : [\"styleModuleUrl\"])]) {\n    const url = new URL((c as unknown as Record<string, string>)[key]!);\n    assert.ok([\"http:\", \"https:\"].includes(url.protocol) && !url.username && !url.password);\n  }\n  assert.equal(c.roots?.length, fixture.roots.length);\n  for (const [index, root] of c.roots.entries()) {\n    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(root).sort(), [\"plugins\", \"rootId\"]);\n    assert.equal(root.rootId, fixture.roots[index]!.rootId);\n    assert.ok(root.plugins.length > 0 && root.plugins.length <= 64);\n    const ids = new Set<string>();\n    for (const plugin of root.plugins) {\n      assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(plugin).sort(), [\"moduleUrl\", \"pluginId\"]);\n      assert.ok(plugin.pluginId.length > 0 && !ids.has(plugin.pluginId)); ids.add(plugin.pluginId);\n      const url = new URL(plugin.moduleUrl); assert.ok([\"http:\", \"https:\"].includes(url.protocol) && !url.username && !url.password);\n    }\n    assert.deepEqual([...ids].sort(), [...fixture.roots[index]!.requiredPlugins].sort());\n  }\n  if (c.renderer === \"react\") {\n    assert.ok(c.sessionFactoryModules && c.sessionFactoryModules.length > 0 && c.sessionFactoryModules.length <= 64);\n    for (const source of c.sessionFactoryModules) {\n      assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(source).sort(), [\"exportName\", \"moduleUrl\"]);\n      assert.match(source.exportName, /^[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*$/);\n      const url = new URL(source.moduleUrl); assert.ok([\"http:\", \"https:\"].includes(url.protocol) && !url.username && !url.password);\n    }\n  }\n  return c;\n}\n\n/** 🎮️ Exercises physical isolation and awaited retirement on actual independently mounted renderers. */\nexport async function runEmbeddedBrowserAcceptance(args: readonly string[]): Promise<void> {\n  const options = new Map<string, string>();\n  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 2) {\n    assert.ok([\"--configuration\", \"--output\"].includes(args[i]!) && args[i + 1] && !options.has(args[i]!));\n    options.set(args[i]!, args[i + 1]!);\n  }\n  assert.ok(options.has(\"--configuration\") && options.has(\"--output\"), \"embedded acceptance requires --configuration and --output\");\n  const config = parseEmbeddedBrowserConfiguration(JSON.parse(readFileSync(resolve(options.get(\"--configuration\")!), \"utf8\")));\n  const output = resolve(options.get(\"--output\")!); mkdirSync(output, { recursive: true });\n  const { chromium } = await import(\"playwright\");\n  const browser = await chromium.launch({ headless: true, args: [\"--enable-unsafe-webgpu\", \"--ignore-gpu-blocklist\", `--use-angle=${process.platform === \"darwin\" ? \"metal\" : process.platform === \"win32\" ? \"d3d11\" : \"vulkan\"}`] });\n  const context = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: fixture.viewport.width, height: fixture.viewport.height }, deviceScaleFactor: fixture.viewport.deviceScaleFactor });\n  const page = await context.newPage(); const logs: string[] = []; const receipts: Receipt[] = []; const workers = new Set<unknown>();\n  const log = (message: string) => { logs.push(message); if (logs.length > 8192) logs.shift(); writeFileSync(resolve(output, `embedded-${config.renderer}.log`), logs.join(\"\\n\") + \"\\n\"); };\n  page.on(\"console\", m => log(`${m.type()} ${m.text()}`)); page.on(\"pageerror\", e => log(`pageerror ${e.message}`));\n  page.on(\"worker\", worker => { if (config.frameWorkerUrl && new URL(worker.url()).pathname === new URL(config.frameWorkerUrl).pathname) { workers.add(worker); worker.on(\"close\", () => workers.delete(worker)); } });\n  const persist = () => { writeFileSync(resolve(output, `embedded-${config.renderer}.json`), JSON.stringify({ schemaVersion: 1, configuration: config, receipts }, null, 2)); writeFileSync(resolve(output, `embedded-${config.renderer}.log`), logs.join(\"\\n\") + \"\\n\"); };\n  try {\n    const harness = new URL(\"__semio_embedded_acceptance__\", config.serve).href;\n    await page.route(harness, route => route.fulfill({ contentType: \"text/html\", body: '<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset=\"utf-8\"></head><body></body></html>' }));\n    await page.goto(harness, { waitUntil: \"domcontentloaded\" });\n    await page.evaluate(() => { localStorage.clear(); document.body.replaceChildren(); document.body.style.cssText = \"margin:0;background:#eee\"; });\n    await page.evaluate(async ({ config, fixture }) => {\n      const state = window as unknown as { embeddedAcceptance: { mounts: Record<string, any>; stale: Record<string, any>; inputs: unknown[]; boot: (index: number) => Promise<void>; focusOwner: () => string | null } };\n      if (config.styleModuleUrl) await import(config.styleModuleUrl);\n      const module = await import(config.libraryModuleUrl);\n      const mounts: Record<string, any> = {}; const stale: Record<string, any> = {}; const inputs: unknown[] = [];\n      for (const spec of fixture.roots) {\n        const root = document.createElement(\"div\"); root.id = spec.rootId; const [x, y, width, height] = spec.rect;\n        root.style.cssText = `position:absolute;left:${x}px;top:${y}px;width:${width}px;height:${height}px;overflow:hidden`; document.body.appendChild(root);\n      }\n      const focusOwner = () => config.renderer === \"react\" ? module.embeddedReactFocusOwner() : document.activeElement?.closest('[id^=\"shell-\"]')?.id ?? null;\n      for (const type of [\"pointerdown\", \"pointerup\", \"keydown\", \"keyup\"]) document.addEventListener(type, event => { const e = event as PointerEvent & KeyboardEvent; inputs.push({ type, trusted: event.isTrusted, root: (event.target as Element)?.closest('[id^=\"shell-\"]')?.id ?? null, admittedRoot: focusOwner(), key: e.key ?? null, x: e.clientX ?? null, y: e.clientY ?? null }); }, true);\n      const boot = async (index: number) => {\n        const spec = fixture.roots[index]!; const plugins = config.roots[index]!.plugins;\n        const frameWorkerUrl = index === 0 && !mounts[spec.rootId] ? undefined : config.frameWorkerUrl;\n        if (mounts[spec.rootId]) stale[spec.rootId] = mounts[spec.rootId];\n        mounts[spec.rootId] = config.renderer === \"wgpu\" ? await module.bootFrameworkOsWgpu({ rootId: spec.rootId, plugin: spec.plugin, plugins, locks: { locale: spec.locale }, rendererModuleUrl: config.rendererModuleUrl, rendererWasmUrl: config.rendererWasmUrl, frameWorkerUrl, suppressAutoIntroduction: spec.suppressAutoIntroduction }) : await module.mountEmbeddedReactOracle({ ...spec, plugins, sessionFactoryModules: config.sessionFactoryModules });\n      };\n      state.embeddedAcceptance = { mounts, stale, inputs, boot, focusOwner };\n      await boot(0); await boot(1);\n    }, { config, fixture });\n    const observe = async (): Promise<State[]> => page.evaluate(async ({ renderer, fixture }) => {\n      const mounted = (window as any).embeddedAcceptance.mounts;\n      return Promise.all(fixture.roots.map(async spec => {\n        const root = document.getElementById(spec.rootId)!; let controls: State[\"controls\"]; let surfaces: string[];\n        if (!root.children.length) return { id: spec.rootId, locale: \"\", ready: false, error: null, intro: false, palette: false, controls: [], surfaces: [], text: \"\", canvases: 0, layout: [0, 0] };\n        if (renderer === \"wgpu\") {\n          const chrome = JSON.parse(await mounted[spec.rootId].introspection.dumpChrome());\n          controls = chrome.hits.map((h: any) => ({ id: h.controlId, rect: h.rect }));\n          surfaces = chrome.surfaces.map((s: any) => `${s.level}:${s.id}`).sort();\n        } else {\n          const box = root.getBoundingClientRect();\n          controls = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(\"[id]\")).map(e => { const r = e.getBoundingClientRect(); return { id: e.id, rect: [r.x - box.x, r.y - box.y, r.width, r.height] }; }).filter(c => c.rect[2]! > 0 && c.rect[3]! > 0);\n          surfaces = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll(\"[data-window-id],[data-level=panel],[data-level=dialog]\")).map(e => e.getAttribute(\"data-window-id\") ?? e.id ?? e.getAttribute(\"data-level\")!).sort();\n        }\n        const ready = root.hasAttribute(\"data-semio-os-ready\") || root.querySelector(\"[data-shell-ready]\") !== null;\n        const layout = root.querySelector(renderer === \"wgpu\" ? \"canvas\" : '[data-slot=\"layout\"]')?.getBoundingClientRect();\n        return { id: spec.rootId, locale: root.lang || root.querySelector(\"[lang]\")?.getAttribute(\"lang\") || \"\", ready, error: root.getAttribute(\"data-semio-os-error\") || root.querySelector(\"[data-shell-error]\")?.getAttribute(\"data-shell-error\") || null, intro: controls.some(c => c.id === fixture.introductionControl), palette: root.querySelector('[role=\"combobox\"]') !== null, controls, surfaces, text: root.textContent ?? \"\", canvases: root.querySelectorAll(\"canvas\").length, layout: [layout?.width ?? 0, layout?.height ?? 0] };\n      }));\n    }, { renderer: config.renderer, fixture });\n    const wait = async (predicate: (states: State[]) => boolean, label: string) => {\n      const deadline = Date.now() + 90_000;\n      do { const states = await observe(); (event.type.startsWith(\"key\") ? event.admittedRoot : null)) === owner), \"physical input reached a foreign shell\");\n      }\n      receipts.push({ phase, before, after, workers: workers.size, input }); persist();\n      await page.screenshot({ path: resolve(output, `${config.renderer}-${phase}.png`) });\n      console.log(`[DEBUG] embedded ${config.renderer} ${phase} accepted workers=${workers.size}`);\n    };\n    const suppress = async (id: string, value: boolean) => page.evaluate(({ id, value }) => (window as any).embeddedAcceptance.mounts[id].setIntroductionSuppressed(value), { id, value });\n    await record(\"boot\", async () => {}, (_, s) => s.every(s => s.ready) && !s[0]!.intro && s[1]!.intro);\n    assert.deepEqual(receipts[0]!.after.map(s => s.locale), fixture.roots.map(s => s.locale));\n    assert.deepEqual(receipts[0]!.after.map(s => s.layout), fixture.roots.map(s => s.rect.slice(2)), \"shell chrome must use its own root dimensions\");\n    if (config.renderer === \"wgpu\") receipts[0]!.evidence = { rootAWorkerUrl: \"public-library-default\", rootBWorkerUrl: config.frameWorkerUrl };\n    assert.equal(workers.size, config.renderer === \"wgpu\" ? 2 : 0);\n    await record(\"release-a-introduction\", () => suppress(\"shell-a\", false), (_, s) => s[0]!.intro && s[1]!.intro);\n    await record(\"suppress-b-introduction\", () => suppress(\"shell-b\", true), (_, s) => s[0]!.intro && !s[1]!.intro);\n    await record(\"suppress-a-introduction\", () => suppress(\"shell-a\", true), (_, s) => s.every(s => !s.intro));\n    for (const [index, id] of [\"shell-a\", \"shell-b\"].entries()) {\n      await record(`pointer-${index === 0 ? \"a\" : \"b\"}`, async () => {\n        const state = (await observe())[index]!; const control = state.controls.find(c => c.id === fixture.settingsControl); assert.ok(control);\n        const root = await page.locator(`#${id}`).boundingBox(); assert.ok(root); const [x, y, width, height] = control.rect;\n        await page.mouse.click(root.x + x! + width! / 2, root.y + y! + height! / 2);\n      }, (b, s) => JSON.stringify(b[index]!.surfaces) !== JSON.stringify(s[index]!.surfaces) && JSON.stringify(b[1 - index]!.surfaces) === JSON.stringify(s[1 - index]!.surfaces));\n      await record(`keyboard-${index === 0 ? \"a\" : \"b\"}`, async () => {\n        const control = (await observe())[index]!.controls.find(c => c.id === fixture.settingsControl); assert.ok(control);\n        const root = await page.locator(`#${id}`).boundingBox(); assert.ok(root); const [x, y, width, height] = control.rect;\n        await page.mouse.click(root.x + x! + width! / 2, root.y + y! + height! / 2);\n        const owner = await page.evaluate(() => (window as any).embeddedAcceptance.focusOwner());\n        assert.equal(owner, id, \"physical focus belongs to the selected shell\");\n        await page.keyboard.press(process.platform === \"darwin\" ? 2 : 0);\n    await record(\"stale-retire-a\", () => page.evaluate(async () => { const old = (window as any).embeddedAcceptance.stale[\"shell-a\"]; await old(); if (old.introspection) { let code = \"\"; try { await old.introspection.dumpChrome(); } catch (e) { code = (e as Error).message; } if (code !== \"wgpu-mount-retired\") throw new Error(\"retired introspection remained live\"); } }), (_, s) => s.every(s => s.ready));\n    await record(\"retire-a\", () => page.evaluate(async () => { const mount = (window as any).embeddedAcceptance.mounts[\"shell-a\"]; await Promise.all([mount(), mount()]); }), (_, s) => !s[0]!.ready && s[0]!.canvases === 0 && s[1]!.ready);\n    assert.equal(await page.locator(\"#shell-a\").evaluate(e => e.children.length), 0); assert.equal(workers.size, config.renderer === \"wgpu\" ? 1 : 0);\n    console.log(`[DEBUG] embedded ${config.renderer} retire-a awaited worker-close and empty owner`);\n    await record(\"retire-b\", () => page.evaluate(async () => { await (window as any).embeddedAcceptance.mounts[\"shell-b\"](); }), (_, s) => s.every(s => !s.ready && s.canvases === 0));\n    assert.equal(await page.locator(\"#shell-b\").evaluate(e => e.children.length), 0); assert.equal(workers.size, 0);\n    console.log(`[DEBUG] embedded ${config.renderer} retire-b awaited worker-close and empty owner`);\n    await page.evaluate(harness => {\n      const iframe = document.createElement(\"iframe\"); iframe.id = \"embedded-iframe\"; iframe.allow = \"webgpu\"; iframe.src = harness;\n      iframe.style.cssText = \"position:absolute;left:20px;top:60px;width:760px;height:880px;border:0\"; document.body.appendChild(iframe);\n    }, harness);\n    const iframe = page.frameLocator(\"#embedded-iframe\"); await iframe.locator(\"body\").waitFor();\n    const frame = page.frames().find(f => f !== page.mainFrame() && f.url() === harness); assert.ok(frame);\n    await frame.evaluate(async ({ config, fixture }) => {\n      const root = document.createElement(\"div\"); root.id = \"iframe-shell\"; root.style.cssText = \"width:760px;height:880px\"; document.body.appendChild(root);\n      const module = await import(config.libraryModuleUrl); const spec = fixture.roots[0]!; const plugins = config.roots[0]!.plugins;\n      if (config.styleModuleUrl) await import(config.styleModuleUrl);\n      (window as any).iframeMount = config.renderer === \"wgpu\" ? await module.bootFrameworkOsWgpu({ rootId: root.id, plugin: spec.plugin, plugins, locks: { locale: spec.locale }, rendererModuleUrl: config.rendererModuleUrl, rendererWasmUrl: config.rendererWasmUrl, frameWorkerUrl: config.frameWorkerUrl, suppressAutoIntroduction: false }) : await module.mountEmbeddedReactOracle({ ...spec, rootId: root.id, plugins, sessionFactoryModules: config.sessionFactoryModules, suppressAutoIntroduction: false });\n    }, { config, fixture });\n    await iframe.locator(\"[data-semio-os-ready],[data-shell-ready]\").first().waitFor({ timeout: 90_000 });\n    await page.waitForTimeout(1500);\n    const iframeIntro = await frame.evaluate(async ({ renderer, control }) => renderer === \"wgpu\" ?
