```json
    "path": "🧰️framework/🛍️products/💻️os/🔨️modules/🧑‍💻dev/🟦️.ts",
    "before": "// #region 🧲️Header\n/** 🖥️ OS dev runner — boots the Rust program framework with a selectable renderer. */\n// #endregion 🧲️Header\n\nimport \"./🎨️.css\";\n\nexport type { PluginBuildTarget } from \"../🔌️plugin/📇️registry/🤖️generated/🧩️plugins/🟦️.ts\";\nexport { PLUGIN_BUILD_TARGETS, EXTENSION_TARGETS, PROGRAM_TARGETS, pluginModuleUrl, extensionModuleUrl } from \"../🔌️plugin/📇️registry/🤖️generated/🧩️plugins/🟦️.ts\";\nexport { PLAYGROUND_SESSION } from \"virtual:semio-playground-session\";\n\nimport type { AppRole } from \"@semio-tech/framework\";\nimport { resolvePlaygroundBoot } from \"@semio-tech/framework\";\nimport { PLUGIN_CATALOG } from \"../🔌️plugin/📇️registry/🟦️.ts\";\nimport { PLAYGROUND_SESSION } from \"virtual:semio-playground-session\";\nimport { resolveShellBrandById } from \"./🏷️brand/🟦️.ts\";\nimport { resolveBootQueryAppRole, resolveBootQueryExampleId } from \"./🔗️boot-query/🟦️.ts\";\n\n/** 🚀️ Boots the selected playground with its owner's browser contributions. */\nexport async function bootFrameworkOsDev(options: { readonly backboneWorkerFactory?: import(\"@semio-tech/framework-renderer-react\").FrameworkOsBootOptions[\"backboneWorkerFactory\"]; readonly documentServices?: import(\"@semio-tech/framework-renderer-react\").FrameworkOsBootOptions[\"documentServices\"]; readonly brands: readonly import(\"@semio-tech/framework\").ShellBrand[]; readonly surfaceSessionFactories?: import(\"@semio-tech/framework-renderer-react\").FrameworkOsBootOptions[\"surfaceSessionFactories\"] }) {\nconst renderer = import.meta.env.VITE_SEMIO_RENDERER ?? import.meta.env.SEMIO_RENDERER ?? \"react\";\nconst boot = resolvePlaygroundBoot(PLUGIN_CATALOG, import.meta.env.VITE_SEMIO_PLUGIN || PLAYGROUND_SESSION.variant, PLAYGROUND_SESSION);\nconst pluginFilter = boot.variant;\n/** 📌️ Pinned app: the serve passes `VITE_SEMIO_APP_ID` as `\"\"` when the playground row declares no\n * `app`, and ShellHost treats any defined `appId` as a pin it must find in the primary manifest — so an\n * empty value must fall through to the playground default, never reach the shell as a pin. */\nconst appId = import.meta.env.VITE_SEMIO_APP_ID || boot.defaultAppId;\n\n/** 👁️✏️ Per-server default surface role (contract §5): `VITE_SEMIO_APP_ROLE` is\n * `\"viewer\"`|`\"editor\"`, default `\"editor\"`. Mirrors `resolveBootAppRole`'s own validation so an\n * unrecognized value falls back rather than throwing. */\nconst envAppRole: AppRole = import.meta.env.VITE_SEMIO_APP_ROLE === \"viewer\" ? \"viewer\" : \"editor\";\n\n/** 👁️✏️ Per-navigation surface role: `?role=viewer`/`?role=editor` wins over the env default, so\n * `http://127.0.0.1:6018/?plugin=<variant>&role=viewer` opens the viewer without restarting the server —\n * the React half of the `?role=` axis the wgpu browser boot already reads. */\nconst appRole: AppRole = typeof window === \"undefined\" ? envAppRole : resolveBootQueryAppRole(window.location.search, envAppRole);\n\n/** 🏷️ Baked-in shell brand for this artifact (registry `brand` column or `SEMIO_BRAND`); no `?query=` override. */\nconst brand = resolveShellBrandById(options.brands, import.meta.env.VITE_SEMIO_BRAND || undefined);\n\n/** 🔒️ Boot-time-only shell preference locks; unlike `program`, these have no `?query=` override. */\nconst locks = {\n  exampleId: import.meta.env.VITE_SEMIO_LOCKED_EXAMPLE || undefined,\n  locale: import.meta.env.VITE_SEMIO_LOCKED_LOCALE || undefined,\n  terminology: import.meta.env.VITE_SEMIO_LOCKED_TERMINOLOGY || undefined,\n  themeId: import.meta.env.VITE_SEMIO_LOCKED_THEME || undefined,\n  appearance: import.meta.env.VITE_SEMIO_LOCKED_APPEARANCE || undefined,\n};\n\n/** 🎛️ Boot-time shell preference defaults — seed values that keep their in-app switcher visible.\n * `?example=` is the per-navigation axis over the `VITE_SEMIO_DEFAULT_EXAMPLE` per-server seed, the\n * React half of the axis the wgpu browser boot reads into its own `active_example_id`, so one url opens\n * the same document on 6018 and 6118. It seeds a DEFAULT, never a lock: the picker stays live. */\nconst defaults = {\n  exampleId: typeof window === \"undefined\" ? import.meta.env.VITE_SEMIO_DEFAULT_EXAMPLE || undefined : resolveBootQueryExampleId(window.location.search, import.meta.env.VITE_SEMIO_DEFAULT_EXAMPLE || undefined),\n};\n\nif (typeof document !== \"undefined\" && document.getElementById(\"root\") != null && !import.meta.vitest) {\n  const plugins = boot.plugins;\n  if (renderer !== \"wgpu\") {\n    const { bootFrameworkOs } = await import(\"@semio-tech/framework-renderer-react\");\n    void bootFrameworkOs({ backboneWorkerFactory: options.backboneWorkerFactory, documentServices: options.documentServices, plugin: pluginFilter, plugins, surfaceSessionFactories: options.surfaceSessionFactories, appId, appRole, locks, defaults, brand }).catch((error) => {\n      console.error(\"[TRACE] os-dev react boot failed\", error);\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n}\n"
  },
  {
    "path": "🧰️framework/🛍️products/💻️os/🔨️modules/📺️renderer/🧑‍🎨engine/🧪️tests/🧭️boot-axis-parity/🦀️.rs",
    "before": "//! 🧭️ Boot-axis parity law — the four boot doors must speak ONE vocabulary.\n//!\n//! React has a single door (`FrameworkOsBootOptions`, `🧱️elements/🐚️Shell/🟦️.tsx`); wgpu has three\n//! (the trunk page's descriptor `🎯️targets/🧊️wgpu/🧭️boot-descriptor/🟦️.ts`, the embeddable\n//! `FrameworkOsWgpuBootOptions` in `🎬️renderer-boot/🟦️.ts`, and the native CLI flags in\n//! `⌨️native-entrypoint/🦀️.rs`). They had drifted into three different subsets of the same axes, so\n//! `?plugin=cad&example=concrete-forest` and `--plugin cad` opened different things. This parses all\n//! four field sets out of their own sources and asserts they name the same axes, modulo an explicit,\n//! reasoned allowlist — a standing law rather than three one-off fixes that can drift apart again\n//! (ticket 26/09/17/WGPU-RENDERER-REACT-PARITY packet W1d, audit packet 10).\n\nuse std::collections::BTreeSet;\nuse std::path::PathBuf;\n\n/// 🌳️ `…/🧑‍🎨engine`, the common root of all four sources.\nfn engine_root() -> PathBuf {\n    PathBuf::from(env!(\"CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR\")).join(\"../../../..\").canonicalize().expect(\"engine root\")\n}\n\nfn read(relative: &str) -> String {\n    let path = engine_root().join(relative);\n    std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap_or_else(|error| panic!(\"read {}: {error}\", path.display()))\n}\n\n/// 🧾️ The `readonly <name>` keys of ONE exported TypeScript object type, in declaration order. The\n/// body is taken from `export type <name> = {` to the first line that is exactly `};`, so a nested\n/// inline object (indented) cannot end it and a sibling type cannot leak into it.\nfn typescript_type_fields(source: &str, type_name: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n    let header = format!(\"export type {type_name} = {{\");\n    let start = source.find(&header).unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"`{type_name}` is declared\")) + header.len();\n    let body = &source[start..];\n    let end = body.find(\"\\n};\").unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"`{type_name}` closes at column 0\"));\n    body[..end]\n        .lines()\n        .filter_map(|line| {\n            let trimmed = line.trim_start();\n            let rest = trimmed.strip_prefix(\"readonly \")?;\n            let name = rest.split(['?', ':']).next()?.trim();\n            (!name.is_empty() && name.chars().all(|character| character.is_ascii_alphanumeric())).then(|| name.to_string())\n        })\n        .collect()\n}\n\n/// 🧾️ The `pub <name>:` fields of ONE Rust struct, camelCased the way its `rename_all` serde\n/// attribute renames them — so the Rust twin is compared in the wire spelling, not the snake one.\nfn rust_struct_fields(source: &str, struct_name: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n    let header = format!(\"pub struct {struct_name} {{\");\n    let start = source.find(&header).unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"`{struct_name}` is declared\")) + header.len();\n    let body = &source[start..];\n    let end = body.find(\"\\n}\").unwrap_or_else(|| panic!(\"`{struct_name}` closes at column 0\"));\n    body[..end]\n        .lines()\n        .filter_map(|line| {\n            let name = line.trim_start().strip_prefix(\"pub \")?.split(':').next()?.trim();\n            (!name.is_empty() && name.chars().all(|character| character.is_ascii_lowercase() || character == '_')).then(|| camel_case(name))\n        })\n        .collect()\n}\n\nfn camel_case(snake: &str) -> String {\n    let mut out = String::with_capacity(snake.len());\n    let mut upper_next = false;\n    for character in snake.chars() {\n        if character == '_' {\n            upper_next = true;\n        } else if upper_next {\n            out.extend(character.to_uppercase());\n            upper_next = false;\n        } else {\n            out.push(character);\n        }\n    }\n    out\n}\n\n/// 🧾️ Every `--flag` the native entrypoint reads through `arg_value`, as a `kebab` name.\nfn native_arg_flags(source: &str) -> Vec<String> {\n    source\n        .match_indices(\"arg_value(\\\"--\")\n        .filter_map(|(index, needle)| {\n            let rest = &source[index + needle.len()..];\n            rest.find('\"').map(|end| rest[..end].to_string())\n        })\n        .collect()\n}\n\n/// 🔤️ One canonical axis name for the four spellings the same axis carries.\nfn canonical(name: &str) -> &'static str {\n    match name {\n        \"plugin\" | \"pluginVariant\" => \"plugin\",\n        \"plugins\" => \"plugins\",\n        \"rootId\" => \"rootId\",\n        \"appId\" | \"app\" => \"appId\",\n        \"appRole\" | \"role\" => \"appRole\",\n        \"appMode\" | \"mode\" => \"appMode\",\n        \"appExample\" | \"example\" => \"appExample\",\n        \"brand\" | \"brandId\" => \"brand\",\n        \"locks\" => \"locks\",\n        \"defaults\" => \"defaults\",\n        \"hub\" => \"hub\",\n        \"user\" => \"hubUser\",\n        \"dataDir\" | \"data-dir\" => \"hubDataDir\",\n        \"brokerProof\" => \"brokerProof\",\n        \"rendererModuleUrl\" => \"rendererModuleUrl\",\n        \"surfaceSessionFactories\" => \"surfaceSessionFactories\",\n        other => Box::leak(other.to_string().into_boxed_str()),\n    }\n}\n\nfn axes(names: impl IntoIterator<Item = String>) -> BTreeSet<&'static str> {\n    names.into_iter().map(|name| canonical(&name)).collect()\n}\n\n/// 🙈️ Axes one door legitimately does not carry, each with the reason it cannot. Nothing else may be\n/// missing: a NEW axis added to one door and not the others fails this law until it is either wired\n/// or listed here with a reason.\n///\n/// 🧭️ The descriptor is the RESOLVED axis set the renderer receives, not a caller's options:\n/// `rootId`/`plugins`/`rendererModuleUrl` address the mount and the module, never the session,\n/// and `surfaceSessionFactories` is a JS closure that cannot cross into wasm at all.\n///\n/// ⚛️ React's door: `appMode` has no React reader yet (the mode axis is `?mode=`, wgpu-only);\n/// `hub`/`hubUser`/`hubDataDir` reach React through ShellHost's backbone worker rather than\n/// through boot options; `brokerProof` is read from `location.hash` at module scope\n/// (`🏛️ShellHost/🟦️.tsx`), never passed in; `appExample` is React's `defaults.exampleId`, which\n/// `defaults` already covers; `rendererModuleUrl` has no React analogue (no wasm renderer\n/// module to point at).\n///\n/// 🧊️ The embeddable wgpu door: `surfaceSessionFactories` is React-only (see above);\n/// `brokerProof` is read from the page's own `location.hash`, like React's, never passed in.\n///\n/// ⌨️ The native CLI: `rootId`/`plugins`/`rendererModuleUrl`/`surfaceSessionFactories` are\n/// browser-mount concepts a winit window has no counterpart for; `brokerProof` is a browser\n/// hash hand-off (native claims its local credential through the inherited fd instead);\n/// `locks`/`defaults` stay process-env axes (`SEMIO_LOCKED_*`/`SEMIO_DEFAULT_EXAMPLE`), the\n/// same per-server level React reads them at (`VITE_SEMIO_LOCKED_*`), so they are deliberately\n/// NOT per-invocation flags.\nfn allowed_absences(door: &str) -> BTreeSet<&'static str> {\n    match door {\n        \"descriptor\" => [\"rootId\", \"plugins\", \"rendererModuleUrl\", \"surfaceSessionFactories\"].into_iter().collect(),\n        \"react\" => [\"appMode\", \"appExample\", \"hub\", \"hubUser\", \"hubDataDir\", \"brokerProof\", \"rendererModuleUrl\"].into_iter().collect(),\n        \"wgpu-library\" => [\"surfaceSessionFactories\", \"brokerProof\"].into_iter().collect(),\n        \"native\" => [\"rootId\", \"plugins\", \"rendererModuleUrl\", \"surfaceSessionFactories\", \"brokerProof\", \"locks\", \"defaults\"].into_iter().collect(),\n        other => panic!(\"unknown door {other}\"),\n    }\n}\n\n/// ⚙️ Native flags that are not boot axes at all — ops probes and the scale-bench harness.\nconst NATIVE_NON_AXIS_FLAGS: [&str; 4] = [\"scale\", \"scale-wasm\", \"report\", \"shards\"];\n\n/// 🌐️ The hub trio is ONE field on the descriptor and three flags on the CLI; naming the two\n/// members here keeps the union honest without pretending the descriptor forgot them.\n#[test]\nfn every_boot_door_names_the_same_axes() {\n    let react = axes(typescript_type_fields(&read(\"🧱️elements/🐚️Shell/🟦️.tsx\"), \"FrameworkOsBootOptions\"));\n    let descriptor_ts = axes(typescript_type_fields(&read(\"🎯️targets/🧊️wgpu/🧭️boot-descriptor/🟦️.ts\"), \"WgpuBootDescriptor\"));\n    let library = axes(typescript_type_fields(&read(\"🎯️targets/🧊️wgpu/🎬️renderer-boot/🟦️.ts\"), \"FrameworkOsWgpuBootOptions\"));\n    let native = axes(native_arg_flags(&read(\"🎯️targets/🧊️wgpu/⌨️native-entrypoint/🦀️.rs\")).into_iter().filter(|flag| !NATIVE_NON_AXIS_FLAGS.contains(&flag.as_str())).collect::<Vec<_>>());\n\n    let mut descriptor_axes = descriptor_ts.clone();\n    if descriptor_axes.contains(\"hub\") {\n        descriptor_axes.extend([\"hubUser\", \"hubDataDir\"]);\n    }\n    let mut library_axes = library.clone();\n    if library_axes.contains(\"hub\") {\n        library_axes.extend([\"hubUser\", \"hubDataDir\"]);\n    }\n\n    let union: BTreeSet<&'static str> = react.iter().chain(descriptor_axes.iter()).chain(library_axes.iter()).chain(native.iter()).copied().collect();\n    for (door, present) in [(\"react\", &react), (\"descriptor\", &descriptor_axes), (\"wgpu-library\", &library_axes), (\"native\", &native)] {\n        let allowed = allowed_absences(door);\n        let missing: Vec<&str> = union.iter().filter(|axis| !present.contains(*axis) && !allowed.contains(*axis)).copied().collect();\n        assert!(missing.is_empty(), \"boot door `{door}` does not carry {missing:?} — wire it, or add it to `allowed_absences` with the reason it cannot exist there\");\n        let stale: Vec<&str> = allowed.iter().filter(|axis| present.contains(*axis)).copied().collect();\n        assert!(stale.is_empty(), \"boot door `{door}` now carries {stale:?}, which `allowed_absences` still excuses — delete the stale allowlist entries\");\n    }\n}\n\n/// 🧭️ The TypeScript descriptor and its Rust twin are ONE shape: a field added to one and not the\n/// other would deserialize as a silent default on the renderer side, which is exactly the class of\n/// drift this packet closed.\n#[test]\nfn the_descriptor_rust_twin_matches_the_typescript_shape() {\n    let typescript: BTreeSet<String> = typescript_type_fields(&read(\"🎯️targets/🧊️wgpu/🧭️boot-descriptor/🟦️.ts\"), \"WgpuBootDescriptor\").into_iter().collect();\n    let renderer = read(\"🎯️targets/🧊️wgpu/🧊️renderer/🦀️.rs\");\n    let rust: BTreeSet<String> = rust_struct_fields(&renderer, \"WgpuBootDescriptor\").into_iter().collect();\n    assert_eq!(typescript, rust, \"`WgpuBootDescriptor` must carry the same fields in 🧭️boot-descriptor/🟦️.ts and 🧊️renderer/🦀️.rs\");\n    for nested in [\"WgpuBootLocks\", \"WgpuBootDefaults\", \"WgpuBootHub\", \"WgpuBootBrand\"] {\n        let typescript: BTreeSet<String> = typescript_type_fields(&read(\"🎯️targets/🧊️wgpu/🧭️boot-descriptor/🟦️.ts\"), nested).into_iter().collect();\n        let rust: BTreeSet<String> = rust_struct_fields(&renderer, nested).into_iter().collect();\n        assert_eq!(typescript, rust, \"`{nested}` must carry the same fields on both sides\");\n    }\n}\n\n/// 📏️ The three doors refuse the same oversized field. A cap that drifts is a url one renderer\n/// accepts and the other rejects.\n#[test]\nfn every_door_bounds_a_field_at_the_same_capacity() {\n    let descriptor_ts = read(\"🎯️targets/🧊️wgpu/🧭️boot-descriptor/🟦️.ts\");\n    assert!(descriptor_ts.contains(\"export const WGPU_BOOT_FIELD_CAPACITY = 2048;\"), \"the TypeScript field cap is 2048\");\n    assert!(descriptor_ts.contains(\"export const WGPU_BOOT_LOCATION_CAPACITY = 8192;\"), \"the TypeScript location cap is 8192\");\n    assert!(read(\"🎯️targets/🧊️wgpu/🧊️renderer/🦀️.rs\").contains(\"pub const WGPU_BOOT_FIELD_CAPACITY: usize = 2048;\"), \"the Rust field cap is the same 2048\");\n    assert!(read(\"../../🧑‍💻dev/🔗️boot-query/🟦️.ts\").contains(\"export const BOOT_QUERY_CAPACITY = 8192;\"), \"React's own query cap is the same 8192\");\n}\n"
  }
]
```

The existing common embedded-mount file contains27 original host lifecycle laws plus2 new closed carrier/locale laws. React whole test registration admits this common suite additively, retaining its existing WGPU registration, so the shared four-door operational contract has pure TypeScript owner admission while global Cargo mutations are unready. No native consumer/normal prepare is launched under that source hold. Whole original additional registry source before:

Left dynamic, the browser's native module loader simply rejects\n// the unresolvable bare specifier at runtime, which the `catch` below already treats as \"unavailable\".\nconst RUST_SYNC_WORKER_MODULE_SPECIFIER = \"@semio-tech/store-worker\";\n\nasync function ensureRustHost(): Promise<RustWorkerHost | null> {\n  if (rustHost) return rustHost;\n  if (typeof WebAssembly === \"undefined\") return null;\n  try {\n    const module = await import(RUST_SYNC_WORKER_MODULE_SPECIFIER);\n    await module.default();\n    rustHost = new module.BackboneWorkerHost() as RustWorkerHost;\n    return rustHost;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nconst rustHostPromise = ensureRustHost();\n\ntype DocumentExecutionOwner = \"typescript\" | \"rust\";\ntype DocumentExecutionOwnerEntry = Readonly<{ owner: DocumentExecutionOwner; documentId: string; clientInstanceId: string; spaceId?: string }>;\n\nconst documentExecutionOwners = new Map<string, DocumentExecutionOwnerEntry>();\n\nfunction validDocumentOpeningAttemptId(value: unknown): value is string {\n  return typeof value === \"string\" && /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-4[0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/u.test(value);\n}\n\nfunction documentRuntimeKeyForConfig(config: ArtifactActorConfig): string {\n  const hub = hubBinding(config);\n  return hub === null ? crypto.randomUUID();\n    if (!validDocumentOpeningAttemptId(clientInstanceId)) return;\n    const next: DocumentExecutionOwner = hubBinding(request) === null && host !== null ? \"rust\" : \"typescript\";\n    const runtimeKey = documentRuntimeKeyForConfig(request);\n    const previous = documentExecutionOwners.get(runtimeKey);\n    if (previous?.clientInstanceId === clientInstanceId) return;\n    if (previous !== undefined) {\n      const close: BackboneWorkerRequest = { kind: \"close\", documentId: request.documentId, clientInstanceId: previous.clientInstanceId, ...(previous.spaceId === undefined ? {} : { spaceId: previous.spaceId }) };\n      if (previous.owner === \"typescript\") typescriptDispatch(close);\n      else rustDispatch(close);\n    }\n    const hub = hubBinding(request);\n    const openedRequest: BackboneWorkerRequest = { ...request, clientInstanceId };\n    documentExecutionOwners.set(runtimeKey, { owner: next, documentId: request.documentId, clientInstanceId, ...(hub === null ? {} : { spaceId: hub.spaceId }) });\n    if (next === \"typescript\") typescriptDispatch(openedRequest);\n    else rustDispatch(openedRequest);\n    return;\n  }\n  if (request.kind === \"send\" || request.kind === \"close\") {\n    const runtimeKey = ownedDocumentRuntimeKey(request.documentId, request.spaceId);\n    if (runtimeKey === null) return;\n    const current = documentExecutionOwners.get(runtimeKey);\n    if (current !== undefined && request.clientInstanceId !== current.clientInstanceId) return;\n    const owner = current?.owner ?? (host === null ? \"typescript\" : \"rust\");\n    if (owner === \"typescript\") typescriptDispatch(request);\n    else rustDispatch(request);\n    if (request.kind === \"close\" && current !== undefined && request.clientInstanceId === current.clientInstanceId) documentExecutionOwners.delete(runtimeKey);\n    return;\n  }\n  if (host === null) typescriptDispatch(request);\n  else rustDispatch(request);\n}\n\nfunction isBackboneWorkerWireMessage(message: unknown): message is BackboneWorkerWireMessage {\n  return typeof message === \"object\" && message !== null && \"wire\" in message && (message as BackboneWorkerWireMessage).wire instanceof Uint8Array;\n}\n\nfunction decodeWorkerRequest(message: BackboneWorkerWireMessage): BackboneWorkerRequest {\n  return \"/semio-backbone\";\nconst CANONICAL_BOOTSTRAP_FOLDER_MIRROR_PATH = `${FOLDER_ENDPOINT_PATH}/canonical-bootstrap`;\n/** 🛟️ Sanity-fallback poll cadence (finding 1): the folder's change stream is the primary wake signal, so this only\n * ever fires while {@link ArtifactState.watchHealthy} is `false` — a slow, jittered self-heal while no stream is open, not\n * the primary path. Jittered per tick (not a fixed `setInterval`) so many documents self-healing together never\n * synchronize into a request burst. */\nconst SANITY_POLL_MIN_MS = 24_000;\nconst SANITY_POLL_MAX_MS = 36_000;\n/** ⏱️ Caps how long any single folder/blob fetch can hang (finding 3) — composed with\n * {@link fetchWithTimeout} so a stalled dev-middleware response can never pin a document forever. */\nconst FOLDER_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000;\nconst BLOB_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS = 15_000;\n/** 🗃️ Bounded local outbound-mutation queue (finding 5) — see {@link rejectMutationQueueOverflow}\n * for the overflow contract: reject and report, never silently drop. */\nconst PENDING_MUTATIONS_QUEUE_LIMIT = 2_000;\nconst ARTIFACT_BOOTSTRAP_DEADLINE_MS = 15_000;\nconst ARTIFACT_BOOTSTRAP_DIAGNOSTIC_MAX_BYTES = 4_096;\nconst EXECUTION_TARGET_DIAGNOSTIC_MAX_BYTES = 1_024;\n// 🔁️ HUB_RECONNECT_MIN_MS/MAX_MS moved to `🟦️.ts`'s `🔖️HubBinding` region (imported above)\n// — single source of truth shared with `DirectoryClient.stream`'s reconnect loop.\n/** ♻️ Coordinator follow-up (finding 4b): how long a hub connection must stay open before a\n * SUBSEQUENT drop is allowed to reset that transport's backoff back near its floor, instead of\n * continuing to grow from whatever `retryWithJitteredBackoff` attempt count it was already on.\n * Deliberately NOT \"the socket opened\" — a server that accepts a connection and immediately drops\n * it in a fast loop must still see the backoff climb (that IS the failure mode the backoff exists\n * for), so the threshold has to be comfortably longer than any such instant-drop cycle. Half of\n * {@link HUB_RECONNECT_MAX_MS} (30s): long enough that no\n * single accept-then-drop attempt could plausibly cross it, short enough that a connection which\n * has been genuinely healthy for a modest stretch still gets credit before its next blip. */\nconst SUSTAINED_HEALTHY_MS = 15_000;\n\n//#endregion 🔖️Constants\n\n//#region 🔖️Reconnect\n/**\n * ♻️ Coordinator follow-up (finding 4b): drives `attempt` (one physical connection's full\n * lifecycle — connect, stay open, eventually close) through {@link retryWithJitteredBackoff}\n * forever, but as a LOOP of fresh calls rather than one long-lived call. `attempt` resolving is\n * this loop's signal that the connection stayed open long enough to count as sustainedly healthy\n * before it (ordinarily) closed — see {@link connectHubOnce} — so the NEXT\n * cycle starts a brand-new {@link retryWithJitteredBackoff} call with its own zeroed internal\n * attempt/backoff state, rather than inheriting a large accumulated delay from earlier, already-\n * resolved blips. `attempt` rejecting (a close before sustained health) is absorbed entirely\n * inside the SAME `retryWithJitteredBackoff` call, so its backoff keeps growing across those —\n * exactly the \"rapid accept-then-drop cycling still backs off\" case the sustained-health gate\n * exists to protect.\n *\n * `retryWithJitteredBackoff`'s own signature has no notion of \"reset now\" — it only stops\n * retrying on success or abort — so this reset cannot be expressed by calling it once; looping\n * fresh calls from here is the only way to get a real reset without editing\n * `🧰️framework/📦️packages/🟦️typescript/🟦️.ts` (outside this packet's owned path).\n */\nasync function reconnectForever(signal: AbortSignal, attempt: () => Promise<void>, minMs: number, maxMs: number): Promise<void> {\n  while (!signal.aborted) {\n    try {\n      await retryWithJitteredBackoff(attempt, { minMs, maxMs, signal });\n    } catch {\n      return; // 🛑 only reachable via abort — `attempt` never lets a real failure escape the retry loop above.\n    }\n  }\n}\n//#endregion 🔖️Reconnect\n\n//#region 🔖️DocumentState\nexport type ArtifactState = {\n  runtimeKey: string;\n  config: ArtifactActorConfig;\n  openClientInstanceId: string;\n  actor: string;\n  hubActorReady: boolean;\n  pendingSocketActorId: string | null;\n  channel: BroadcastChannel;\n  socket: WebSocket | null;\n  presenceAuthority: Readonly<{ socket: WebSocket; scope: DocumentScope; verifiedSurfaceId: string }> | null;\n  /** 🛑️ Aborted once, in {@link closeArtifact} — cancels every in-flight folder/blob fetch this\n   * document owns and unblocks any pending {@link retryWithJitteredBackoff} delay for its hub/SSE\n   * reconnect loops immediately (finding 3). Never re-created; a closed document stays closed. */\n  docAbort: AbortController;\n  /** 🪪️ The private verified execution-target owner for a non-`react` hub target, live only while\n   * this document's plan/socket authority is. Never posted, cloned or encoded. */\n  executionTargetOpen: symbol | null;\n  executionTargetLease: DocumentExecutionTargetLease | null;\n  browserActorReservation: DocumentBrowserActorReservation | null;\n  /** 📥️ Remote backbone messages of an actor-bound document that arrived before its actor child was reserved — the hub's\n   * `Welcome` tail comes before the `Session` that admits the child — handed to the child in arrival order the moment it is\n   * reserved. Bounded like the child's own pre-binding retention. */\n  browserActorBackboneBeforeReservation: Uint8Array[];\n  /** 🔀️ Another human's operations were folded in while this shell's own operations were still unacknowledged. The hub\n   * orders those remote operations BEFORE the pending local ones, but the local fold applied them after, so once the local\n   * ones are accepted the document is rebuilt from the hub's authoritative pair: measured as two humans each showing the\n   * OTHER's value for the same field forever after a short link loss (🎫️ 26/09/23 C10). */\n  remoteFoldedOverLocal: boolean;\n  /** 🔌️ The mounted child's hub link — the kernel's one `DocumentLink` state machine (`documentLinkTransition`,\n   * `🏪️store/🧫️fixtures/document-link-shortage-v1`): a short loss suspends it, past the bound it expires. */\n  link: DocumentLink;\n  /** 🔌️ Fires at the suspended link's expiry ({@link documentLinkExpiresAtMs}) and retires the child once it expired. */\n  linkShortageTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null;\n  /** ⏳️ The hub refused a batch for a transient reason ({@link hubTransientApplyRefusalV1}): the batch went back to the front of\n   * the outbox, every later batch waits behind it, and the outbox drains one batch of at most `batchLimit` envelopes after\n   * `backoffMs` (doubled per refusal, capped). Cleared by the next `Accepted`. */\n  transientRefusal: { timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null; backoffMs: number; batchLimit: number } | null;\n  browserActorViewState: ResolvedPluginViewState | null;\n  /** 🛟️ Handle for the recursive, jittered sanity-poll reschedule (finding 1) — a plain\n   * `ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>`, not `setInterval`, because each tick schedules its OWN next\n   * delay with fresh jitter rather than ticking on a fixed period. */\n  sanityPollTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null;\n  /** 📡️ Explicit \"is the folder's change stream open\" flag — set when the `backbone.folder` stream opens (fresh or\n   * resumed), cleared when it ends; {@link startSanityPolling} reads it to decide whether a tick revalidates. */\n  watchHealthy: boolean;\n  /** 🥇️ Single-flight folder revalidation (finding 1) — built once per document with\n   * {@link latestWins} over {@link pollFolderOnce}, so the SSE `onmessage` wake, the sanity-poll\n   * tick, and an `externalChanged` local message all share the SAME in-flight guard and can never\n   * stack overlapping reads. null;\n}\n//#endregion 🔖️DocumentState\n\n//#region 🔖️ConfigLane\n/** 🎚️ Canonical `documentId`/`schema` for the OS-wide `os.config.opening` facet (contract freeze\n * §4 of `.🧬semio/🦑️repo/🎫️tickets/🎆️26/🌙️08/☀️16/ARTIFACT-VIEWERS-AND-EDITORS-PER-SUBSET/`) — one\n * singleton instance per install, so the schema id doubles as its document id. */\nexport const OPENING_PREFERENCES_SCHEMA = \"os.config.opening\";\n\n/** 🎚️ Builds the {@link ArtifactActorConfig} that opens the opening-preferences facet through\n * this SAME generic actor mechanism every other document uses — `bindings: []` is not a stub, it\n * IS the \"persisted local-only\" lane (contract freeze §4): {@link folderBinding}/{@link hubBinding}\n * both return `null` on an empty `bindings` array, so `openArtifact` below already skips folder\n * watch and hub websocket setup for this config entirely — no schema-specific branch exists or is\n * needed anywhere in this file for that to hold. */\nexport function openingPreferencesActorConfig(actor: string): ArtifactActorConfig {\n  return { documentId: OPENING_PREFERENCES_SCHEMA, schema: OPENING_PREFERENCES_SCHEMA, bindings: [], actor };\n}\n\n/** 🧮️ Reduces one {@link ArtifactEvent} onto a materialized `OpeningPreferences` — event-sourced,\n * never a mutable map (contract freeze §4). serviceId: string}>[]): void {\n  const wanted = new Set(services.map(({owner, serviceId}) => JSON.stringify([owner, serviceId])));\n  for (const [key, contribution] of documentServiceFactoriesV1) {\n contribution.serviceId, contribution.factory);\n  }\n}\n\n//#region 🔖️WireBridge\n/** 🧮️ A stable, deterministic 32-bit seed for an actor id string, for `WireMutationEnvelope.\n * timestamp.actor` — the TS twin of the Rust actor's `actor_seed` (`framework/sync/rs/lib.rs`\n * `🔖️WireBridge`). Not cryptographic, just a cheap deterministic fold — matches the Rust side's own\n * `DefaultHasher`-based approach in spirit (both are wire-local ordering metadata, never round-\n * tripped back into an app-level {@link MutationEnvelope}). */\nfunction actorSeed(actor: string): number {\n  let hash = 0;\n  for (let index = 0; index < actor.length; index++) {\n    hash = (Math.imul(hash, 31) + actor.charCodeAt(index)) | 0;\n  }\n  return hash >>> 0;\n}\n\n/** ⏰️ Advances `state.hlcCounter` and stamps a fresh wire timestamp for an outbound envelope —\n * the TS twin of the Rust actor's `next_timestamp`. */\nfunction nextWireTimestamp(state: ArtifactState): WireMutationEnvelope[\"timestamp\"] {\n  state.hlcCounter += 1;\n  return { actor: actorSeed(state.actor), physical_ms: Date.now(), logical: state.hlcCounter };\n}\n\n/** #⃣ A cheap, non-cryptographic FNV-1a-style digest for {@link toWireEnvelope}'s placeholder\n * `payloadHash` on the way back through {@link fromWireEnvelope}. String(envelope.mutation_id) : String(envelope.id ?? \"\");\n}\n\nfunction toWireEnvelope(envelope: MutationEnvelope, timestamp: WireMutationEnvelope[\"timestamp\"], actor = envelope.actor): WireMutationEnvelope {\n  return {\n    mutation_id: envelope.id,\n    document_id: envelope.document,\n    actor,\n    dependencies: [...(envelope.deps ?? [])],\n    observed: null,\n    target: [],\n    diff: { schema: envelope.diff.schemaId, payload: encodePackPayload(envelope.diff.payload) },\n    inverse: { schema: envelope.inverse.inverseDiff.schemaId, payload: encodePackPayload(envelope.inverse.inverseDiff.payload) },\n    timestamp,\n    transaction: envelope.transaction ?? null,\n    verb: envelope.verb ?? null,\n  };\n}\n\n/** 🌉️ The inverse of {@link toWireEnvelope} — the TS twin of the Rust actor's `from_wire_envelope`.\n * `baseVersion` is recovered from the payload's own `sequenceNumber` (this actor's payloads are\n * always edit-shaped JSON), mirroring the Rust side's identical recovery. */\nfunction fromWireEnvelope(envelope: WireMutationEnvelope | ExactWireMutationEnvelope): MutationEnvelope {\n  const payload = decodePackPayload(envelope.diff.payload);\n  const sequenceNumber = payload !== null && typeof payload === \"object\" && !Array.isArray(payload) && !isPackInteger(payload) && \"sequenceNumber\" in payload ? {} : { transaction: envelope.transaction }),\n    ...(envelope.verb === null ? {} : { verb: envelope.verb }),\n  };\n}\n\n/** 🌉️ Document-backbone twin of {@link fromWireEnvelope}: a Rust store's events carry opaque\n * `OpBinary` operation or history-transition payloads this worker relays and persists but never\n * interprets, so they stay exact bytes instead of being decoded as pack values. */\nfunction opaqueEnvelopeFromWire(envelope: ExactWireMutationEnvelope): MutationEnvelope {\n  const payload = envelope.diff.payload.slice();\n  return {\n    id: envelope.mutation_id,\n    actor: envelope.actor,\n    document: envelope.document_id,\n    schemaVersion: envelope.diff.schema,\n    deps: [...envelope.dependencies],\n    payloadHash: placeholderPayloadHash(payload),\n    diff: { schemaId: envelope.diff.schema, payload },\n    inverse: { targetOperation: envelope.mutation_id, inverseDiff: { schemaId: envelope.inverse.schema, payload: envelope.inverse.payload.slice() }, baseVersion: 0, dependencies: [], undoPolicy: \"exactBaseOnly\" },\n    ...(envelope.transaction === null ? {} : { transaction: envelope.transaction }),\n    ...(envelope.verb === null ? {} : { verb: envelope.verb }),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction exactWireEnvelope(envelope: WireMutationEnvelope): ExactWireMutationEnvelope {\n  const exactU64 = (value: number, field: string): bigint => {\n    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(value) || value < 0) throw new Error(`wire envelope: ${field} is not an exact u64`);\n    return BigInt(value);\n  };\n  return {\n    mutation_id: envelope.mutation_id,\n    document_id: envelope.document_id,\n    actor: envelope.actor,\n    dependencies: envelope.dependencies,\n    observed: envelope.observed,\n    target: envelope.target,\n    diff: { schema: envelope.diff.schema, payload: Uint8Array.from(envelope.diff.payload) },\n    inverse: { schema: envelope.inverse.schema, payload: Uint8Array.from(envelope.inverse.payload) },\n    timestamp: {\n      actor: exactU64(envelope.timestamp.actor, \"timestamp.actor\"),\n      physical_ms: exactU64(envelope.timestamp.physical_ms, \"timestamp.physical_ms\"),\n      logical: exactU64(envelope.timestamp.logical, \"timestamp.logical\"),\n    },\n    transaction: envelope.transaction,\n    verb: envelope.verb,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction documentBackboneMessage(envelopes: readonly ExactWireMutationEnvelope[]): Uint8Array {\n  return encodeBackboneMessage({ kind: \"mutations\", envelopes: encodeDocumentBackboneEnvelopeBatchExact(envelopes) });\n}\n\nfunction documentBackboneMessageFromDomain(state: ArtifactState, envelopes: readonly MutationEnvelope[]): Uint8Array {\n  return documentBackboneMessage(envelopes.map((envelope) => state.exactLocalEnvelopes.get(envelope)?.envelope ?? exactWireEnvelope(toWireEnvelope(envelope, nextWireTimestamp(state)))));\n}\n\nfunction emitMutationEvent(state: ArtifactState, envelopes: readonly MutationEnvelope[]): void {\n  if (envelopes.length === 0) return;\n  if (hubBinding(state.config)) emitEvent(state, { kind: \"documentBackbone\", message: documentBackboneMessageFromDomain(state, envelopes) });\n  else emitEvent(state, { kind: \"remoteMutations\", envelopes });\n}\n\n/** 🎨️ Stamps `state`'s hub-assigned session color and canonical surface onto an outbound\n * `ArtifactPresencePeer` right before `encodePresencePeer` — the ONE place either field is ever\n * filled; shells never set `peer.color`/`peer.surface` themselves (contract-freeze §C7.4). toolRun: _localToolRun, historyEdit: _localHistoryEdit, ...rest } = stamped;\n  return {\n    ...rest,\n    ...(ephemeral !== null && ephemeral.presence.length > 0 ? { presencePack: ephemeral.presence } : {}),\n    ...(ephemeral !== null && ephemeral.interaction.length > 0 ? { interaction: decodePresenceInteraction(Uint8Array.from(ephemeral.interaction), [0]) } : {}),\n    ...(ephemeral !== null && ephemeral.toolRun.length > 0 ? { toolRun: decodePresenceToolRun(Uint8Array.from(ephemeral.toolRun)) } : {}),\n    ...(ephemeral !== null && ephemeral.historyEdit.length > 0 ? { historyEdit: extra method is added locally instead of widening the shared interface for everyone. */\ntype BinaryFetchTimeoutResponse = FetchTimeoutResponse & { arrayBuffer(): Promise<ArrayBuffer> };\n\nfunction folderEnvelopeUrl(binding: Extract<PersistenceBinding, { kind: \"folder\", dataClass: \"persistedLocalOnly\" }>, documentId: string): string {\n  return Extract<PersistenceBinding, { kind: \"folder\", dataClass: \"persistedLocalOnly\" }>;\n  documentId: string;\n  epoch: number;\n  capability: string;\n}>;\n\nfunction folderCanonicalBootstrapMirrorUrl(owner: Pick<FolderCanonicalBootstrapMirrorOwner, \"binding\" | \"documentId\">, action: \"reserve\" | \"stage\" | \"publish\" | \"retire\"): string {\n  return `${CANONICAL_BOOTSTRAP_FOLDER_MIRROR_PATH}/${action}?uri=${encodeURIComponent(`folder://${owner.binding.path}`)}&documentId=${encodeURIComponent(owner.documentId)}`;\n}\n\nfunction ArtifactState, binding: Extract<PersistenceBinding, { kind: \"folder\", dataClass: \"persistedLocalOnly\" }>, archive: readonly number[]): Promise<void> {\n  await fixed byte authority\");\n  decodeDocumentArchiveBytes(bytes);\n  const response = await fetchWithTimeout(\n    folderEnvelopeUrl(binding, state.config.documentId),\n    { method: \"PUT\", headers: { {\n    requestBaseUrl: \"\",\n    socketGrantIssuer: issuer,\n    request: browserDirectoryRequest,\n  });\n  directoryClient = client;\n  directoryStream = client.stream(since, (message: \"directory-message\", message }),\n    () => {\n      if (scopedDirectoryStreams.get(key) !== stream) return;\n      scopedDirectoryStreams.delete(key);\n      // 🧯️ A scoped 4401 is an authoritative !== null) post(response);\n}\n\n/** 🚨️ `Some(status)` for a {@link DirectoryHttpError}-shaped rejection (the hub answered and\n * rejected the read — authz/validation, never retried); `undefined` for (error as { status: number }).status : undefined;\n}\n\n/** 🚨️ Classifies one rejection into the closed transport vocabulary without touching server text. */\nfunction directoryCommandErrorCode(error: unknown, aborted: boolean): DirectoryCommandErrorCodeV1 {\n  if (aborted) return \"cancelled\";\n  if (error instanceof DirectoryCommandError) return error.code;\n  if (error instanceof DirectoryHttpError) return \"invalid\";\n  return \"transport\";\n}\n\n/** 🆔️ Seals one request, admits it against the fixed capacity, and starts its transport turn. A\n * sealing failure, a duplicate live id, and a full transport are all terminal — never queued. */\nasync function submitDirectoryCommand(requestId: string, command: DirectoryCommand): Promise<void> {\n  if (directoryCommandOperations.has(requestId)) {\n    post({ kind: \"directory-command-failed\", requestId, code: \"request-conflict\" });\n    return;\n  }\n  if (directoryCommandOperations.size >= DIRECTORY_COMMAND_TRANSPORT_CAPACITY) {\n    post({ kind: \"directory-command-failed\", requestId, code: \"capacity\" });\n    return;\n  }\n  let request: DirectoryCommandRequestV1;\n  try {\n    request = sealDirectoryCommandRequestV1(requestId, command);\n    directoryCommandRequestJson(request);\n  } catch {\n    post({ kind: \"directory-command-failed\", requestId, code: \"invalid\" });\n    return;\n  }\n  const operation: DirectoryCommandTransportOperationV1 = { request, abort: new AbortController(), sessionEpoch: directorySessionEpoch, workerEpoch: directoryWorkerEpoch, settled: false };\n  directoryCommandOperations.set(requestId, operation);\n  directoryCommandQueue.push(operation);\n  post(directoryStatus());\n  await flushDirectoryQueue();\n}\n\n/** 🛑️ Cancels one live operation's HTTP wait. The hub may already have linearized the command, so\n * the owner is told `cancelled` (indeterminate) and the id is never silently reissued. */\nfunction cancelDirectoryCommand(requestId: string): void {\n  const operation = directoryCommandOperations.get(requestId);\n  if (!operation) return;\n  operation.abort.abort(new DirectoryCommandError(\"cancelled\"));\n  settleDirectoryCommand(operation, { kind: \"directory-command-failed\", requestId, code: \"cancelled\" });\n  post(directoryStatus());\n}\n\n/** ♻️ Drives the FIFO on every live signal from the stream. (preferenceLane !== lane) return;\n      const authority = browserSessionAuthority;\n      if (authority === null || page.sessionBindingSha256 !== authority.sessionBindingSha256 || page.authorizationGeneration !== authority.authorizationGeneration) {\n        post({ kind: \"preference-lane-failed\", requestId: null, code: \"unauthorized\", retryable: false });\n        closePreferenceLane();\n        return;\n      }\n      lane.after = page.throughSeqInclusive;\n      post({ kind: \"preference-lane-page\", userId: lane.userId, afterSeqExclusive: page.afterSeqExclusive, throughSeqInclusive: page.throughSeqInclusive, hasMore: page.hasMore, events: page.events });\n      if (page.hasMore) lane.again = true;\n    } while (lane.again && preferenceLane === lane);\n  } catch (error) {\n    if (preferenceLane !== lane || lane.abort.signal.aborted) return;\n    const unauthorized = error instanceof DirectoryHttpError && error.status === 401;\n    post({ kind: \"preference-lane-failed\", requestId: null, code: unauthorized ? \"unauthorized\" : \"transport\", retryable: !unauthorized });\n  } finally {\n    lane.fetching = false;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction openPreferenceLane(baseUrl: string, userId: string, after: number): void {\n  closePreferenceLane();\n  const client = new DirectoryClient(baseUrl, {\n    requestBaseUrl: \"\",\n PreferenceLaneV1 = { userId, client, abort: new AbortController(), after, fetching: false, again: false, stream: null };\n  preferenceLane = lane;\n  void fetchPreferenceLanePages(lane);\n  lane.stream = client.streamAcknowledged(after, (message) => {\n    if (preferenceLane !== lane) return;\n    const head = message.kind === \"event\" ? message.event.seq : message.kind === \"heartbeat\" ? message.headSeq : message.kind === \"rebootstrap-required\" ? It is NOT a second socket and NOT a generic queue: it\n * owns exactly one canonical page, at most one in-flight command request/receipt, and at most one\n * one-shot invite capability. Administration mutations never auto-retry: an\n * indeterminate transport becomes `failed` (\"unknown outcome / refresh required\"), because only an\n * exact server receipt may advance the pane. */\nconst DIRECTORY_ADMINISTRATION_CAPACITY = 1;\n\ntype DirectoryAdministrationOperationV1 = {\n  readonly operationEpoch: number;\n  readonly spaceId: string;\n  readonly sessionEpoch: number;\n  readonly workerEpoch: number;\n  abort: AbortController;\n  phase: DirectoryAdministrationPhaseV1;\n  canonicalJson: string | null;\n  page: DirectorySpaceAdministrationPageV1 | null;\n  pageRead: AbortController | null;\n  receiptSha256: string | null;\n  outcome: DirectoryCommandOutcomeV1 | null;\n  inviteToken: string | null;\n  inviteCapabilityStatus: \"available\" | \"copying\" | \"failed\" | null;\n  inviteTransferEpoch: number | null;\n  nextInviteTransferEpoch: number;\n  requestId: string | null;\n  authorPage: boolean;\n  closed: boolean;\n};\n\nlet directoryAdministration: DirectoryAdministrationOperationV1 | null = null;\n\n/** 📣️ Publishes the operation's complete renderer-visible state; never its transport internals. */\nfunction postDirectoryAdministrationState(operation: DirectoryAdministrationOperationV1, code?: DirectoryCommandErrorCodeV1): void {\n  post({\n    kind: \"directory-administration-state\",\n    operationEpoch: operation.operationEpoch,\n    spaceId: operation.spaceId,\n    phase: operation.phase,\n    ...(operation.canonicalJson === null ? {} : { canonicalJson: operation.canonicalJson }),\n    ...(operation.receiptSha256 === null ? {} : { receiptSha256: operation.receiptSha256 }),\n    ...(operation.outcome === null ? {} : { outcome: operation.outcome }),\n    ...(code === undefined ? {} : { code }),\n    ...(operation.inviteToken === null ? {} : { inviteCapabilityPending: true }),\n    ...(operation.inviteCapabilityStatus === null ? {} : { inviteCapabilityStatus: operation.inviteCapabilityStatus }),\n  });\n}\n\n/** 🧯️ Erases page, receipt, and capability, then reports one terminal phase exactly once. */\nfunction terminateDirectoryAdministration(operation: DirectoryAdministrationOperationV1, phase: \"cancelled\" | \"denied\" | \"stale\" | \"failed\", code?: DirectoryCommandErrorCodeV1, notify = true): void {\n  if (operation.closed) return;\n  operation.closed = true;\n  operation.abort.abort(new Error(\"directory administration closed\"));\n  operation.pageRead?.abort(new Error(\"directory administration closed\"));\n  operation.pageRead = null;\n  operation.canonicalJson = null;\n  operation.page = null;\n  operation.receiptSha256 = null;\n  operation.outcome = null;\n  operation.inviteToken = null;\n  operation.inviteCapabilityStatus = null;\n  operation.inviteTransferEpoch = null;\n  operation.requestId = null;\n  operation.authorPage = false;\n  operation.phase = phase;\n  if (directoryAdministration === operation) directoryAdministration = null;\n  if (notify) postDirectoryAdministrationState(operation, code);\n}\n\n/** ⛔️ Confirms deletion only after the exact space page is missing. operation.pageRead !== read) return;\n    if (read.signal.aborted) {\n      terminateDirectoryAdministration(operation, \"cancelled\", \"cancelled\");\n      return;\n    }\n    if postDirectoryAdministrationState(operation, \"capacity\");\n    return;\n  }\n  const client = directoryClient;\n  if (client === null) {\n    terminateDirectoryAdministration(operation, \"failed\", (!operation.authorPage) {\n      operation.inviteToken = null;\n      operation.inviteCapabilityStatus = null;\n      operation.inviteTransferEpoch = null;\n    }\n    operation.phase = \"ready\";\n === read) operation.pageRead = null;\n  }\n}\n\n/** 🆕️ Installs the one retained operation for exactly one space, replacing any predecessor. */\nfunction openDirectoryAdministration(operationEpoch: operation: DirectoryAdministrationOperationV1 = {\n    operationEpoch,\n    spaceId,\n    sessionEpoch: directorySessionEpoch,\n    workerEpoch: directoryWorkerEpoch,\n    abort: new AbortController(),\n    phase: \"loading\",\n    canonicalJson: null,\n    page: null,\n    pageRead: null,\n    receiptSha256: null,\n    outcome: null,\n    inviteToken: null,\n    inviteCapabilityStatus: null,\n    inviteTransferEpoch: null,\n    nextInviteTransferEpoch: 0,\n    requestId: null,\n    authorPage: false,\n    closed: false,\n  };\n  directoryAdministration = \"cancelled\");\n}\n//#endregion 🔖️SpaceAdministration\n\n\n\n//#region 🔖️BlobCache\n/** 📦️ Must match `framework/os/core/js/index.ts`'s `BLOB_ENDPOINT_PATH`. A hub-backed fallback\n * (for documents synced through a hub rather than a dev folder) is 0G's job once that route exists —\n * this worker only ever talks to the dev middleware today. */\nconst BLOB_ENDPOINT_PATH = \"/semio-blob\";\n\nconst BLOB_CACHE_DB_NAME = \"semio-blob-cache\";\nconst BLOB_CACHE_DB_VERSION = 1;\nconst BLOB_CACHE_STORE_NAME = \"semio-blobs\";\nconst BLOB_CACHE_LAST_ACCESSED_INDEX = \"lastAccessedAt\";\n/** 💾️ IndexedDB eviction budget for the browser blob cache. 512 MiB comfortably fits a working set of\n * document media (images/audio/small video clips) without risking the browser's own storage-pressure\n * eviction of the whole origin; raise this once real usage data says otherwise. */\nconst BLOB_CACHE_BUDGET_BYTES = 512 * 1024 * 1024;\n\ntype CachedBlobRecord = { hash: string; mediaType: string; size: number; bytes: ArrayBuffer; lastAccessedAt: number };\n\nfunction idbRequest<T>(request: IDBRequest): Promise<T> {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result as T);\n    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error ?? new Error(\"indexeddb request failed\"));\n  });\n}\n\nfunction openBlobCacheDb(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {\n  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {\n    const request = indexedDB.open(BLOB_CACHE_DB_NAME, BLOB_CACHE_DB_VERSION);\n    request.onupgradeneeded = () => {\n      const db = request.result;\n      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(BLOB_CACHE_STORE_NAME)) {\n        const store = db.createObjectStore(BLOB_CACHE_STORE_NAME, { keyPath: \"hash\" });\n        store.createIndex(BLOB_CACHE_LAST_ACCESSED_INDEX, \"lastAccessedAt\");\n      }\n    };\n    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result);\n    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error ?? new Error(\"failed to open blob cache database\"));\n  });\n}\n\nlet blobCacheDbPromise: Promise<IDBDatabase> | null = null;\nfunction blobCacheDb(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {\n  if (!blobCacheDbPromise) blobCacheDbPromise = openBlobCacheDb();\n  return blobCacheDbPromise;\n}\n\n/** 🧮️ Running cache size, lazily seeded from a full scan on first use and kept in sync by\n * {@link writeCachedBlob}/eviction from then on — avoids a cursor sum on every put. */\nlet cachedTotalBytes: number | null = null;\n\nasync function blobCacheTotalBytes(db: IDBDatabase): Promise<number> {\n  if (cachedTotalBytes != null) return cachedTotalBytes;\n  const tx = db.transaction(BLOB_CACHE_STORE_NAME, \"readonly\");\n  const records = await idbRequest<CachedBlobRecord[]>(tx.objectStore(BLOB_CACHE_STORE_NAME).getAll());\n  cachedTotalBytes = records.reduce((sum, record) => sum + record.size, 0);\n  return cachedTotalBytes;\n}\n\n/** ♻️ Evicts least-recently-accessed entries (via the `lastAccessedAt` index, ascending order) until\n * the running total drops back under {@link BLOB_CACHE_BUDGET_BYTES}. */\nasync function evictBlobCacheOverBudget(db: IDBDatabase): Promise<void> {\n  let total = await blobCacheTotalBytes(db);\n  if (total <= BLOB_CACHE_BUDGET_BYTES) return;\n  const tx = db.transaction(BLOB_CACHE_STORE_NAME, \"readwrite\");\n  const index = tx.objectStore(BLOB_CACHE_STORE_NAME).index(BLOB_CACHE_LAST_ACCESSED_INDEX);\n  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {\n    const cursorRequest = index.openCursor();\n    cursorRequest.onsuccess = () => {\n      const cursor = cursorRequest.result;\n      if (!cursor || total <= BLOB_CACHE_BUDGET_BYTES) {\n        resolve();\n        return;\n      }\n      const record = cursor.value as CachedBlobRecord;\n      total -= record.size;\n      cursor.delete();\n      cursor.continue();\n    };\n    cursorRequest.onerror = () => reject(cursorRequest.error ?? (await blobCacheTotalBytes(db))) + record.size;\n  await evictBlobCacheOverBudget(db);\n}\n\n/** 📥️ Reads a blob by hash — cache-first (bumping `lastAccessedAt` for LRU), falling back to the dev\n * server's `GET ${BLOB_ENDPOINT_PATH}/:hash` on a miss and populating the cache. Nothing outside this\n * worker calls this yet (no plugin/UI surface consumes blobs today), so it stays internal rather than\n * growing {@link BackboneWorkerRequest}/{@link BackboneWorkerResponse} with variants nothing sends. */\nasync function getCachedBlob(hash: string): Promise<{ bytes: Uint8Array; mediaType: string } | null> {\n  const cached = await readCachedBlob(hash);\n  if (cached) {\n    void writeCachedBlob({ ...cached, lastAccessedAt: Date.now() });\n    return { bytes: new Uint8Array(cached.bytes), mediaType: cached.mediaType };\n  }\n  // ⏱️ Finding 3: no per-document abort context exists here (the blob cache is global, not tied to\n  // one document), so a fixed timeout is the whole story — still enough that a stalled dev-server\n  // response can't hang this call forever.\n  const response = (await fetchWithTimeout(`${BLOB_ENDPOINT_PATH}/${encodeURIComponent(hash)}`, undefined, { timeoutMs: BLOB_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS })) as BinaryFetchTimeoutResponse;\n  if (response.status === 404) return null;\n  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`blob fetch failed (${response.status})`);\n  const mediaType = response.headers.get(\"content-type\") ?? \"application/octet-stream\";\n  const buffer = await response.arrayBuffer();\n  await writeCachedBlob({ hash, mediaType, size: buffer.byteLength, bytes: buffer, lastAccessedAt: Date.now() });\n  return { bytes: new Uint8Array(buffer), mediaType };\n}\n\n/** 📤️ Writes a blob to the dev server's content-addressed store, caching it locally under the hash the\n * server returns (content-addressing means the caller can't pick the cache key up front). */\nasync function putCachedBlob(bytes: Uint8Array, mediaType: string): Promise<string> {\n  const response = await fetchWithTimeout(\n    `${BLOB_ENDPOINT_PATH}?mediaType=${encodeURIComponent(mediaType)}`,\n    { method: \"PUT\", headers: { \"content-type\": \"application/octet-stream\" }, body: new Uint8Array(bytes) },\n    { timeoutMs: BLOB_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS },\n  );\n  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`blob put failed (${response.status})`);\n  const { hash } = (await response.json()) as { hash: string };\n  await writeCachedBlob({ hash, mediaType, size: bytes.byteLength, bytes: bytes.slice().buffer, lastAccessedAt: Date.now() });\n  return hash;\n}\n\n// 🧷️ Referenced defensively so `getCachedBlob`/`putCachedBlob` aren't flagged unused before a\n// plugin/UI surface calls into them — both are the intended entry points once one does.\nvoid getCachedBlob;\nvoid putCachedBlob;\n//#endregion 🔖️BlobCache\n\n//#region 🔖️Lifecycle\n/** 🌱️ A just-opened document's runtime state: nothing linked, nothing pending, no pair, no lease. null,\n    sessionColor: null,\n    pendingBatches: new Map(),\n    nextBatchId: 0,\n    ingestedMutationIds: new Set(),\n    hlcCounter: 0,\n    closed: false,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction openArtifact(request: ArtifactActorConfig & { readonly clientInstanceId?: string }): void {\n  const config: ArtifactActorConfig = request;\n  const hub = hubBinding(config);\n  const runtimeKey = documentRuntimeKeyForConfig(config);\n  retireArtifactBeforeReplacement(runtimeKey);\n  const channel = new BroadcastChannel(`semio-doc-${runtimeKey}`);\n  const state = newArtifactState(config, case \"queueTransition\":\n                dependencies.queueOutbox(state, [transitionEnvelope(scenario.documentId, String(dispatch.mutationId))]);\n                break;\n              case });\n\n  describe(\"@semio-tech/framework-os PackValueCodec dynamic integers\", () => {\n    function bytesToHex(bytes: Uint8Array): string {\n      return Array.from(bytes)\n        .map((byte) => byte.toString(16).padStart(2, \"0\"))\n        .join(\"\");\n    }\n    function hexToBytes(hex: string): Uint8Array {\n      const out = new Uint8Array(hex.length / 2);\n      for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i++) out[i] = Number.parseInt(hex.substring(i * 2, i * 2 + 2), 16);\n      return out;\n    }\n    // 🔣️ The shared, language-neutral corpus both hosts read — see\n    // `💻️os/🧫️fixtures/🎒️pack-dynamic-integer-v1`. Its `wireHex` is generated by an independent\n    // BigInt/LEB128 oracle and asserted by the native law\n    // `pack_wire_value_preserves_integer_variants_at_u64_i64_boundaries`.\n    function corpusValue(node: Record<string, any>): PackValue {\n      if (typeof node.uint === \"string\") return packUInt(BigInt(node.uint));\n      if (typeof node.int === \"string\") return packInt(BigInt(node.int));\n      if (typeof node.f64LeHex === \"string\") return new DataView(hexToBytes(node.f64LeHex).buffer).getFloat64(0, true);\n      if (Array.isArray(node.list)) return node.list.map(corpusValue);\n      if (Array.isArray(node.map)) return Object.fromEntries((node.map as [string, Record<string, any>][]).map(([key, value]) => [key, corpusValue(value)]));\n      throw new Error(`unsupported corpus node ${JSON.stringify(node)}`);\n    }\n\n    it(\"validates the neutral corpus against its own schema\", async () => {\n      const [{ default: corpus }, { default: schema }] = await Promise.all([import(\"../../🧫️fixtures/🎒️pack-dynamic-integer-v1/🔣️.json\"), import(\"../../🔨️modules/🎒️pack/🌱️value/🧬️schema/🔣️.json\")]);\n      const packValueExport = semioSchemaAjvV1({ strict: true, allErrors: true }).addSchema(schema).getSchema(`${(schema as { $id: string }).$id}#/$defs/PackDynamicIntegerV1`)!;\n      expect(packValueExport(corpus)).toBe(true);\n      expect(corpus.accept).toHaveLength(6);\n      expect(corpus.reject.map((row: { id: string }) => row.id)).toEqual([\"truncated-u64\", \"u64-overflow\", \"nonminimal-u64\", \"nonminimal-zigzag-i64\"]);\n    });\n\n    it(\"encodes and decodes every accepted corpus row byte-exactly with its exact carrier kind\", async () => {\n      const { default: corpus } = await import(\"../../🧫️fixtures/🎒️pack-dynamic-integer-v1/🔣️.json\");\n      for (const row of corpus.accept) {\n        const value = corpusValue(row.value);\n        expect(bytesToHex(encodePackValue(value)), row.id).toBe(row.wireHex);\n        const decoded = decodePackValue(hexToBytes(row.wireHex));\n        expect(decoded, row.id).toEqual(value);\n        if (row.variant === \"uint\" || row.variant === \"int\") {\n          expect(isPackInteger(decoded)).toBe(true);\n          expect((decoded as PackInteger).kind).toBe(row.variant);\n          const magnitude = row.value[row.variant];\n          if (typeof magnitude !== \"string\") throw new Error(\"integer corpus magnitude is missing\");\n          expect((decoded as decodePackValue(bytes);\n          if (!row.decodes) throw new Error(\"noncanonical corpus decoded value is missing\");\n          expect(decoded, row.id).toEqual(corpusValue(row.decodes));\n
